###############################################################################
# Health events
#
# Namespace: health
# Faction: Generic
#
# The everyday wear on body and mind: chronic conditions, illness, wounds, old
# age and recovery, the voices a possessed character hears, the antics of mad
# rulers, the drowned dreams of the north, and Mittens the spymaster.
#
#   1-12     Chronic conditions: syphilis, leprosy, lunacy, possession, stress,
#            depression, and their cures
#   13-19    Illness: falling ill, recovering, sickly children, pneumonia,
#            lover's pox
#   20-21    Wounds: healing, and infection
#   25-26    Old age: infirmity and incapacity
#   30-35    Recovery from the old plagues (only without the Reapers DLC)
#   100-117  The voice from on high: a possessed character of a sane faith is
#            urged to virtue by a voice they take for their god. It is Tzeentch.
#   118-122  The twin-tailed omen that follows the voice from on high
#   123-141  The daemon's voice: a possessed Chaos worshipper or cultist is
#            urged to sin
#   150-158  Lunatic laws a mad ruler passes, and how the realm takes them
#   160-163  Random madness: a gopher maid, howling at Morrslieb, a horse
#            chancellor
#   170-178  Drowned dreams: a raving northerner follows dreams to the Sea of
#            Claws and meets the thing beneath
#   180-185  Mittens the spymaster
#   190-198  The singing sands: a raving southerner of Araby, Sudenburg,
#            Tilea, Estalia or Sartosa follows the desert's song to a city of
#            black glass
#   210-218  The dreaming temple: a raving Old World colonist in Lustria
#            follows the stars to a slann's dream
#
# Fired from:
#   on_yearly_pulse     health list (00_on_actions.txt): 1-14, 21, 25, 26;
#                       health_wounds list (wh_on_actions.txt): 20
#   on_bi_yearly_pulse  health_voices list (wh_on_actions.txt): 100-140
#   on_five_year_pulse  health_madness and health_drowned_dreams lists
#                       (wh_on_actions.txt): 150, 160-162, 170-173, 190-193,
#                       210-213
#   yearly pets list    180 (00_on_actions.txt)
#   MTTH                15-18, 30-35 (short recoveries, is_sick pre-trigger)
#   Elsewhere           19 (seduction and harem effects), 15 (assassins),
#                       20 (wound treatment), 25 (DR_misc), 14 (pregnancy,
#                       ancestor worship), 8 (S_chaos), 163 (lunatic events)
#
# Long-lived races roll less often on the odds of a mortal lifetime: see
# common/scripted_triggers/wh_lifespan_triggers.txt.
#
# Also in this file (each part keeps its own header and namespace):
#   Lunacy events (namespace lunacy, was WHG_lunacy_events)
#   Maimed events (namespace maimed, was rip_maimed_events)
#   Physique events (namespace physique, was HFP_health_events)
###############################################################################

namespace = health

#######################################
# Chronic conditions 1-12
#######################################

# health.1 Syphilis
# An unchaste character, or one whose spouse is infected, catches syphilis.
# Only without the Reapers DLC, which has its own disease system.
# Scope: ROOT is the character.
character_event = {
	id = health.1
	desc = EVTDESChealth1
	picture = GFX_evt_illness
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_yearly_pulse, health list

	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes
	lacks_dlc = "Reapers"

	trigger = {
		health_mortal_body_trigger = yes
		NOR = { trait = syphilitic trait = celibate trait = infirm }
		OR = {
			NOT = { trait = chaste }
			spouse = { is_alive = yes is_abroad = no trait = syphilitic }
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		days = 1
		modifier = { factor = 0.9 kislev_northern_blood_trigger = yes } # Northern blood (WHG_bulwark)
		modifier = { factor = 10 spouse = { trait = syphilitic } }
		modifier = { factor = 2 in_command = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = lustful }
		modifier = { factor = 1.7 trait = hedonist }
		modifier = { factor = 0.2 trait = chaste }
		modifier = { factor = 0.2 lifespan_dwarf_trigger = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 0.08 lifespan_elf_trigger = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 0.25 lifespan_other_long_lived_trigger = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 0.05 lifespan_hardy_race_trigger = yes }
	}

	option = { # Curse it
		name = EVTOPTAhealth1
		add_trait = syphilitic
		blessing_removes_disease_effect = yes
		deaths_gift_removes_disease_effect = yes
		nurgle_blessing_check_effect = yes
	}
}

# health.2 Syphilis fades
# A syphilitic character in good health recovers. Only without Reapers.
# Scope: ROOT is the character.
character_event = {
	id = health.2
	desc = EVTDESChealth2
	picture = GFX_evt_recovery
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_yearly_pulse, health list

	min_age = 16
	is_sick = yes
	capable_only = yes
	lacks_dlc = "Reapers"

	trigger = {
		health = 6
		trait = syphilitic
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		days = 1
		modifier = { factor = 2 health = 8 }
		modifier = { factor = 2 health = 9 }
	}

	option = { # Recovered
		name = EVTOPTAhealth2
		remove_trait = syphilitic
	}
}

# health.3 Leprosy
# A character in poor health catches leprosy. With Reapers, the court
# physician is told and the leper's treatment chain starts.
# Scope: ROOT is the character.
character_event = {
	id = health.3
	desc = EVTDESChealth3
	picture = GFX_evt_illness
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_yearly_pulse, health list

	min_age = 10
	capable_only = yes

	trigger = {
		health_mortal_body_trigger = yes
		NOT = { trait = leper }
		NOT = { health = 5 }
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		days = 1
		modifier = { factor = 0.9 kislev_northern_blood_trigger = yes } # Northern blood (WHG_bulwark)
		modifier = { factor = 2 NOT = { health = 4 } }
		modifier = { factor = 2 in_command = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 2 any_courtier = { trait = leper } }
		modifier = { factor = 2 is_weak_trigger = yes }
		modifier = {
			factor = 2
			OR = { trait = infection trait = severely_injured trait = wounded }
		}
		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = charitable }
		modifier = { factor = 0.2 lifespan_dwarf_trigger = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 0.08 lifespan_elf_trigger = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 0.25 lifespan_other_long_lived_trigger = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 0.05 lifespan_hardy_race_trigger = yes }
	}

	option = { # Unclean
		name = EVTOPTAhealth3
		add_trait = leper
		nurgle_blessing_check_effect = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_dlc = "Reapers" }
			set_character_flag = new_leper_character
			if = {
				limit = { is_playable = no }
				host = {
					random_courtier_or_vassal = {
						limit = {
							liege = { character = PREVPREV }
							has_minor_title = title_court_physician
						}
						character_event = { id = treatment.32 days = 1 }
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				random_courtier_or_vassal = {
					limit = { has_minor_title = title_court_physician }
					character_event = { id = treatment.32 days = 1 }
				}
			}
			character_event = { id = treatment.64 days = 20 }
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				100 = { character_event = { id = symptom.42 days = 365 random = 50 } }
				75 = { character_event = { id = symptom.42 days = 1500 random = 300 } }
				30 = { character_event = { id = symptom.42 days = 3000 random = 600 } }
			}
		}
	}
}

# health.4 Leprosy cured
# A leper in fair health pays a healer of Shallya 25 gold and is cured, unless
# supernatural events are off.
# Scope: ROOT is the leper.
character_event = {
	id = health.4
	desc = EVTDESChealth4
	picture = GFX_evt_recovery
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_yearly_pulse, health list

	min_age = 16
	is_sick = yes
	capable_only = yes

	trigger = {
		health = 4
		treasury = 25
		trait = leper
		NOT = { has_game_rule = { name = supernatural_events value = off } }
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		days = 1
	}

	option = { # Pay the healer
		name = EVTOPTAhealth4
		remove_trait = leper
		treasury = -25
	}
}

# health.5 Lunacy
# A character slips into the first stage of lunacy (lunatic_1, counter 5).
# Fever, prison, paranoia, zeal and a coma that ends in madness each have
# their own text. The lunacy events in WHG_health_events.txt carry on from here.
# Scope: ROOT is the character.
character_event = {
	id = health.5
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESChealth5
		trigger = {
			is_ill = no
			prisoner = no
			NOR = { trait = paranoid trait = zealous trait = incapable }
		}
	}
	desc = { text = EVTDESChealth5b trigger = { trait = paranoid NOT = { trait = incapable } } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESChealth5c trigger = { is_ill = yes NOT = { trait = incapable } } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESChealth5d trigger = { trait = zealous NOT = { trait = incapable } } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESChealth5e trigger = { prisoner = yes NOT = { trait = incapable } } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESChealth5f trigger = { trait = incapable } }
	picture = GFX_evt_lunatic
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_yearly_pulse, health list

	min_age = 10

	trigger = {
		is_lunatic_trigger = no
		lunacy_can_go_mad_trigger = yes
		OR = {
			# The senile and comatose old do not go mad (old by their race's measure); immortals get no pass
			lifespan_age_60_trigger = no
			immortal = yes
			NOT = { trait = incapable }
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		days = 1
		modifier = { factor = 0.9 kislev_northern_blood_trigger = yes } # Northern blood (WHG_bulwark)
		# Long-lived races: per-year odds scaled to their lifespan, and a little hardier
		modifier = { factor = 0.2 lifespan_dwarf_trigger = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 0.08 lifespan_elf_trigger = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 0.25 lifespan_other_long_lived_trigger = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 0.05 lifespan_hardy_race_trigger = yes }
		modifier = { # 12 years on average
			factor = 333
			OR = { trait = syphilitic trait = has_aztec_disease }
		}
		modifier = { # 24 years on average
			factor = 167
			prisoner = yes
			has_character_modifier = the_oubliette
		}
		modifier = { # 80 years on average
			factor = 50
			OR = { trait = has_typhoid_fever trait = has_typhus }
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 10
			prisoner = yes
			NOT = { has_character_modifier = the_oubliette }
			NOT = { has_character_modifier = house_arrest }
		}
		modifier = { # 800 years on average
			factor = 5
			is_ill = yes
			NOR = {
				trait = has_typhoid_fever
				trait = has_typhus
				trait = syphilitic
				trait = has_aztec_disease
			}
		}
		modifier = { factor = 40 trait = rabies } # 1 year on average; rabies also counts as ill
		modifier = { factor = 40 trait = incapable } # A coma from a blow to the head ends in madness
		modifier = { factor = 40 trait = incapable immortal = yes } # Immortals wake from a coma madder still
		modifier = { factor = 4 trait = paranoid }
		modifier = { factor = 4 trait = stressed }
		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = depressed }
		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = zealous }
		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = inbred }
		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = imbecile }
		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = genius }
		modifier = { factor = 1.333 trait = drunkard }
	}

	option = {
		name = {
			text = EVTOPTAhealth5
			trigger = {
				is_ill = no
				prisoner = no
				NOR = { trait = paranoid trait = zealous trait = incapable }
			}
		}
		name = { text = EVTOPTAhealth5b trigger = { trait = paranoid NOT = { trait = incapable } } }
		name = { text = EVTOPTAhealth5c trigger = { is_ill = yes NOT = { trait = incapable } } }
		name = { text = EVTOPTAhealth5d trigger = { trait = zealous NOT = { trait = incapable } } }
		name = { text = EVTOPTAhealth5e trigger = { prisoner = yes NOT = { trait = incapable } } }
		name = { text = EVTOPTAhealth5f trigger = { trait = incapable } }
		add_trait = lunatic_1
		set_variable = { which = "lunatic_counter" value = 5 }
		if = { limit = { trait = depressed } remove_trait = depressed }
		if = { limit = { trait = stressed } remove_trait = stressed }
		if = { limit = { trait = incapable } remove_trait = incapable }
	}
}

# health.6 Lunacy fades
# A twitchy lunatic in fair health, without a disease that rots the mind,
# comes back to their senses.
# Scope: ROOT is the lunatic.
character_event = {
	id = health.6
	desc = EVTDESChealth6
	picture = GFX_evt_recovery
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_yearly_pulse, health list

	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes

	trigger = {
		health = 4
		trait = lunatic_1
		NOR = { trait = syphilitic trait = has_aztec_disease }
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		days = 1
		modifier = { factor = 2 health = 6 }
	}

	option = { # Clear-headed again
		name = EVTOPTAhealth6
		remove_trait = lunatic_1
		set_variable = { which = "lunatic_counter" value = 0 }
	}
}

# health.7 Possession
# Something takes hold of a character's soul. Followers of the gods of Araby
# blame a djinn. Daemons and mindless beings cannot be possessed. The voices
# (health.100 and 123) follow.
# Scope: ROOT is the character.
character_event = {
	id = health.7
	desc = { text = EVTDESChealth7 trigger = { NOT = { religion_group = ormazd_gods_group } } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESChealth7b trigger = { religion_group = ormazd_gods_group } }
	picture = GFX_evt_lunatic
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_yearly_pulse, health list

	min_age = 10
	capable_only = yes

	trigger = {
		NOT = { trait = possessed }
		lunacy_can_go_mad_trigger = yes
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		days = 1
		modifier = { factor = 1.1 NOT = { prestige = 20 } }
		modifier = { factor = 1.1 NOT = { piety = 10 } }
		modifier = { factor = 1.1 trait = zealous }
		modifier = { factor = 2 religion_group = chaos_gods_group }
		modifier = { factor = 2 is_devil_worshiper_trigger = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 0.2 lifespan_dwarf_trigger = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 0.08 lifespan_elf_trigger = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 0.25 lifespan_other_long_lived_trigger = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 0.05 lifespan_hardy_race_trigger = yes }
	}

	option = { # Something is in me
		name = { text = EVTOPTAhealth7 trigger = { NOT = { religion_group = ormazd_gods_group } } }
		name = { text = EVTOPTAhealth7b trigger = { religion_group = ormazd_gods_group } }
		add_trait = possessed
	}
}

# health.8 Possession ends
# The possessing spirit lets go. Every voice, omen and possession flag is
# cleared. Also fired by WHG_daemon_events.txt when a daemon is driven out.
# Scope: ROOT is the possessed character.
character_event = {
	id = health.8
	desc = { text = EVTDESChealth8 trigger = { religion_group = old_world_group } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESChealth8b trigger = { religion_group = ormazd_gods_group } }
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESChealth8c
		trigger = { NOR = { religion_group = ormazd_gods_group religion_group = old_world_group } }
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_recovery
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_yearly_pulse, health list; WHG_daemon_events.txt

	min_age = 10
	capable_only = yes

	trigger = {
		health = 4
		trait = possessed
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		days = 1
		modifier = { factor = 1.1 prestige = 20 }
		modifier = { factor = 1.1 piety = 10 }
	}

	immediate = {
		remove_character_modifier = voice_of_greater_daemon
		remove_character_modifier = voice_of_tzeentch
		remove_character_modifier = holy_comet
		remove_character_modifier = military_jesus
		remove_character_modifier = speaking_in_tounges
		remove_character_modifier = lycanthropy
		clr_character_flag = demon_child
		clr_character_flag = demon_child_fake
		clr_character_flag = child_exorcism
		clr_character_flag = possessed_by_khornate
		clr_character_flag = possessed_by_nurglite
		clr_character_flag = possessed_by_slaaneshi
		clr_character_flag = possessed_by_tzeentchian
	}

	option = { # Free
		name = { text = EVTOPTAhealth8 trigger = { religion_group = old_world_group } }
		name = { text = EVTOPTAhealth8b trigger = { NOT = { religion_group = old_world_group } } }
		remove_trait = possessed
	}
}

# health.9 Stress
# The weight of rule, council work or a regency wears a character down.
# Scope: ROOT is the character.
character_event = {
	id = health.9
	desc = EVTDESChealth9
	picture = GFX_evt_bad_news
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_yearly_pulse, health list

	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes

	trigger = {
		lunacy_can_go_mad_trigger = yes
		OR = { is_ruler = yes is_councillor = yes has_minor_title = title_regent }
		NOR = { trait = stressed trait = infirm }
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		days = 1
		modifier = { factor = 1.333 trait = diligent }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 in_command = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 0.1 trait = slothful }
	}

	option = { # Too much to do
		name = EVTOPTAhealth9
		add_trait = stressed
	}
}

# health.10 Stress eases
# A stressed character shakes it off, far sooner once they hold no office.
# Scope: ROOT is the character.
character_event = {
	id = health.10
	desc = EVTDESChealth10
	picture = GFX_evt_recovery
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_yearly_pulse, health list

	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = stressed
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		days = 1
		modifier = {
			factor = 200
			is_ruler = no
			is_councillor = no
			in_command = no
			NOT = { has_minor_title = title_regent }
		}
		modifier = { factor = 1.333 is_ruler = no }
		modifier = { factor = 1.333 is_councillor = no }
		modifier = { factor = 1.333 in_command = no }
		modifier = { factor = 1.333 NOT = { has_minor_title = title_regent } }
	}

	option = { # Breathe again
		name = EVTOPTAhealth10
		remove_trait = stressed
	}
}

# health.11 Depression
# A character sinks into gloom: orphans, cynics, the stressed, the sick and
# the ugly most of all.
# Scope: ROOT is the character.
character_event = {
	id = health.11
	desc = EVTDESChealth11
	picture = GFX_evt_bad_news
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_yearly_pulse, health list

	min_age = 10
	capable_only = yes

	trigger = {
		lunacy_can_go_mad_trigger = yes
		NOR = { trait = depressed trait = zealous trait = content }
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		days = 1
		modifier = { factor = 2 NOT = { age = 20 } mother_even_if_dead = { is_alive = no } }
		modifier = { factor = 1.33 NOT = { age = 20 } father_even_if_dead = { is_alive = no } }
		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = cynical }
		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = stressed }
		modifier = { factor = 2 is_ill = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 1.4 trait = slothful }
		modifier = { factor = 1.4 has_ugly_trait_trigger = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = ambitious }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 in_command = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = diligent }
	}

	option = { # Why bother
		name = EVTOPTAhealth11
		add_trait = depressed
	}
}

		days = 1

		modifier = { factor = 10 trait = zealous }
		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = diligent }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = cynical }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = stressed }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 is_ill = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 0.666 trait = slothful }
		modifier = { factor = 0.666 has_ugly_trait_trigger = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = ambitious }
		modifier = { factor = 0.75 in_command = yes }
	}

	option = { # The sun again
		name = EVTOPTAhealth12
		remove_trait = depressed
	}
}

# health.13 Falling ill
# The character catches a plain illness. Fired by health.14 without Reapers.
# Scope: ROOT is the character.
character_event = {
	id = health.13
	desc = EVTDESChealth13
	picture = GFX_evt_illness
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	is_triggered_only = yes # health.14

	option = { # To bed
		name = EVTOPTAhealth13
		add_trait = ill
		blessing_removes_disease_effect = yes
		deaths_gift_removes_disease_effect = yes
		nurgle_blessing_check_effect = yes
	}
}

# health.14 Illness strikes
# Hidden. A healthy character falls ill: a Reapers symptom, or health.13
# without Reapers. Gloom, stress, a weak body and a damp cell make it likelier.
# Also fired by pregnancy and ancestor worship events.
# Scope: ROOT is the character.
character_event = {
	id = health.14
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_yearly_pulse, health list

	trigger = {
		health_mortal_body_trigger = yes
		health = 3.0
		is_ill = no
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		days = 1
		modifier = { factor = 0.9 kislev_northern_blood_trigger = yes } # Northern blood (WHG_bulwark)
		# Long-lived races: per-year odds scaled to their lifespan, and a little hardier
		modifier = { factor = 0.2 lifespan_dwarf_trigger = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 0.08 lifespan_elf_trigger = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 0.25 lifespan_other_long_lived_trigger = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 0.05 lifespan_hardy_race_trigger = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = depressed }
		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = stressed }
		modifier = { factor = 2 is_weak_trigger = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 1.33 prisoner = yes has_character_modifier = the_oubliette }
		modifier = { factor = 1.1 prisoner = yes has_character_modifier = the_dungeon }
		modifier = { factor = 0.666 base_health = 6 }
		modifier = { factor = 0.666 is_strong_trigger = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 base_health = 7 }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 base_health = 8 }
	}

	immediate = {
		if = { limit = { has_dlc = "Reapers" } add_symptom_effect = yes }
		else = { character_event = { id = health.13 } }
	}
}

# health.15 Illness passes
# An ill character recovers, faster in a strong body. Also fired by the
# assassins events after a poisoning fails.
# Scope: ROOT is the character.
character_event = {
	id = health.15
	desc = EVTDESChealth15
	picture = GFX_evt_recovery
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_sick = yes

	trigger = {
		base_health = 3
		trait = ill
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 12
		modifier = { factor = 0.9 kislev_northern_blood_trigger = yes } # Northern blood (WHG_bulwark)
		modifier = { factor = 2.0 trait = depressed }
		modifier = { factor = 2.0 trait = stressed }
		modifier = { factor = 0.3 base_health = 5 }
		modifier = { factor = 0.2 base_health = 6 }
		modifier = { factor = 0.1 base_health = 7 }
	}

	option = { # On my feet
		name = EVTOPTAhealth15
		remove_trait = ill
	}
}

# health.17 Pneumonia
# An ill character's illness settles in the lungs, most often in winter and
# on campaign. Only without Reapers.
# Scope: ROOT is the character.
character_event = {
	id = health.17
	desc = EVTDESChealth17
	picture = GFX_evt_illness
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	is_sick = yes
	lacks_dlc = "Reapers"

	trigger = {
		health_mortal_body_trigger = yes
		OR = { trait = ill trait = has_measles }
		NOT = { trait = pneumonic }
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 240
		modifier = { factor = 1.1 kislev_northern_blood_trigger = yes } # Northern blood (WHG_bulwark)
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 NOT = { base_health = 5 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 NOT = { base_health = 4 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = depressed }
		modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = stressed }
		modifier = { factor = 2.0 base_health = 6 }
		modifier = { factor = 2.0 base_health = 7 }
		modifier = { factor = 2.0 base_health = 8 }
		modifier = { # Winter campaigns
			factor = 0.25
			NOT = { month = 2 }
			in_command = yes
		}
	}

	option = { # Every breath hurts
		name = EVTOPTAhealth17
		add_trait = pneumonic
		blessing_removes_disease_effect = yes
		deaths_gift_removes_disease_effect = yes
		nurgle_blessing_check_effect = yes
	}
}

		base_health = 4
		trait = wounded
		NOT = { trait = infection }
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		days = 1
		modifier = { factor = 2 base_health = 5.1 }
		modifier = { factor = 2.5 base_health = 6 }
	}

		name = EVTOPTAhealth20
		remove_trait = wounded
		add_scarred_tiered_effect = yes
	}

# health.21 The wound festers
# A wounded character's wound goes bad: an infection with Reapers, a plain
# illness without.
# Scope: ROOT is the character.
character_event = {
	id = health.21
	desc = EVTDESChealth21
	picture = GFX_evt_illness
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_yearly_pulse, health list

	trigger = {
		health_mortal_body_trigger = yes
		trait = wounded
		NOT = { base_health = 8 }
		is_ill = no
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		days = 1
		modifier = { factor = 1.1 in_command = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = leper }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 NOT = { base_health = 6 } }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 lifespan_age_60_trigger = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 lifespan_age_70_trigger = yes }
	}

	option = { # Fetch the surgeon
		name = EVTOPTAhealth21
		if = {
			limit = { has_dlc = "Reapers" }
			add_trait = infection
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = treatment.2 days = 365 random = 1095 } }
		}
		else = {
			add_trait = ill
			blessing_removes_disease_effect = yes
			deaths_gift_removes_disease_effect = yes
			nurgle_blessing_check_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

#######################################
# Old age 25-26
#######################################

# health.25 Infirmity
# Old age catches up with a mortal. Dwarfs are spared before 200 and elves
# before 500, and every long-lived or hardy race rolls far less often.
# Immortals, Chaos chosen and Grail Knights never grow infirm. Also fired by
# DR_misc_events.txt.
# Scope: ROOT is the character.
character_event = {
	id = health.25
	desc = EVTDESChealth25
	picture = GFX_evt_illness
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_yearly_pulse, health list; DR_misc_events.txt

	min_age = 45
	capable_only = yes

	trigger = {
		health_mortal_body_trigger = yes
		NOT = { trait = infirm }
		immortal = no
		is_chaos_chosen_trigger = no
		NOT = { trait = grail_knight }
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		days = 1
		# Dwarfs do not grow infirm before 200, elves before 500
		modifier = { factor = 0 lifespan_dwarf_trigger = yes NOT = { age = 200 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0 lifespan_elf_trigger = yes NOT = { age = 500 } }
		# Long-lived races: per-year odds scaled to their lifespan, and a little hardier
		modifier = { factor = 0.2 lifespan_dwarf_trigger = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 0.08 lifespan_elf_trigger = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 0.25 lifespan_other_long_lived_trigger = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 0.05 lifespan_hardy_race_trigger = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 lifespan_age_60_trigger = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 1.4 lifespan_age_70_trigger = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 2 lifespan_age_80_trigger = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 2 lifespan_age_90_trigger = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = cancer }
		modifier = { factor = 2 is_weak_trigger = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 1.3 NOT = { base_health = 5.0 } }
		modifier = { factor = 1.3 NOT = { base_health = 4.0 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 base_health = 5.1 }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 base_health = 6.0 }
	}

	option = { # The years tell
		name = EVTOPTAhealth25
		add_trait = infirm
	}
}

# health.26 Incapacity
# An infirm or inbred mortal can no longer rule or think clearly.
# Scope: ROOT is the character.
character_event = {
	id = health.26
	desc = EVTDESChealth26
	picture = GFX_evt_recovery
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_yearly_pulse, health list

	trigger = {
		health_mortal_body_trigger = yes
		OR = { trait = infirm trait = inbred }
		NOT = { trait = incapable }
		immortal = no
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		days = 1
		modifier = { factor = 2 NOT = { health = 3.0 } }
		modifier = { factor = 2 NOT = { base_health = 5.0 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 NOT = { trait = infirm } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 base_health = 5.1 }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 base_health = 6.0 }
		modifier = { factor = 0.2 lifespan_dwarf_trigger = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 0.08 lifespan_elf_trigger = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 0.25 lifespan_other_long_lived_trigger = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 0.05 lifespan_hardy_race_trigger = yes }
	}

	option = { # Nothing left
		name = EVTOPTAhealth26
		add_trait = incapable
		remove_trait = infirm
	}
}

#######################################
# The voice from on high 100-117
# A possessed character of a sane faith hears a voice they take for their
# god. It urges virtue and charity, then sends an omen. It is Tzeentch, and
# the omen leads to heresy and folly (118-122). The modifier is named for the
# truth: voice_of_tzeentch, shown only to those who can see religious traits.
#######################################

# health.100 The voice from on high
# A possessed character of a sane faith, other than a greenskin or a Chaos
# cultist, begins to hear a gentle voice. Pious and mystic souls hear it
# soonest. About once a century of possession.
# Scope: ROOT is the possessed character.
character_event = {
	id = health.100
	desc = EVTDESChealth100
	picture = GFX_evt_whispers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_bi_yearly_pulse, health_voices list

	only_playable = yes
	min_age = 10
	capable_only = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = possessed
		health_no_voice_trigger = yes
		lunacy_sane_faith_trigger = yes
		NOT = { religion_group = greenskin_gods_group }
		is_devil_worshiper_trigger = no
		NOT = { has_game_rule = { name = supernatural_events value = off } }
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		days = 1
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 NOT = { piety = 0 } }
		modifier = { factor = 1.1 piety = 100 }
		modifier = { factor = 1.1 piety = 250 }
		modifier = { factor = 1.1 piety = 500 }
		modifier = { factor = 1.1 piety = 1000 }
		modifier = { factor = 1.33 trait = zealous }
		modifier = { factor = 0.8 trait = cynical }
		modifier = { factor = 1.1 trait = crusader }
		modifier = { factor = 1.1 trait = celibate }
		modifier = { factor = 0.8 trait = hedonist }
		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = mystic }
	}

	option = { # My god speaks to me
		name = EVTOPTAhealth100
		add_character_modifier = { name = voice_of_tzeentch duration = -1 }
	}
}

# health.102 The hair shirt
# The voice bids the character wear a hair shirt in penance. Courtiers find
# it unbecoming.
# Scope: ROOT is the possessed character.

	id = health.102

	}

#######################################
# The daemon's voice 123-140
# A possessed Chaos worshipper, hidden cultist or impious character hears a
# daemon urging them to sin. Each sin belongs to one of the Dark Gods.
#######################################

# health.123 The daemon's voice
# A possessed Chaos worshipper, a member of a Chaos cult, or a character with
# negative piety begins to hear a daemon. Cultists hear it soonest. About once
# a century of possession.
# Scope: ROOT is the possessed character.
character_event = {
	id = health.123
	desc = EVTDESChealth123
	picture = GFX_evt_whispers
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_bi_yearly_pulse, health_voices list

	only_playable = yes
	min_age = 10
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no

	trigger = {
		trait = possessed
		health_no_voice_trigger = yes
		NOT = { religion_group = greenskin_gods_group }
		OR = {
			religion_group = chaos_gods_group
			is_devil_worshiper_trigger = yes
			NOT = { piety = 0 }
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		days = 1
		modifier = { factor = 3 is_devil_worshiper_trigger = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 2 NOT = { piety = 0 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 piety = 100 }
		modifier = { factor = 0.8 trait = zealous }
		modifier = { factor = 1.1 trait = cynical }
		modifier = { factor = 0.8 trait = celibate }
		modifier = { factor = 1.1 trait = hedonist }
		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = mystic }
	}

	option = { # I am listening
		name = EVTOPTAhealth123
		add_character_modifier = { name = voice_of_greater_daemon duration = -1 }
	}
}

# health.124 Khorne: the prisoner
# The daemon wants a prisoner to bleed. One of the character's prisoners
# loses health.
# Scope: ROOT is the possessed character.
character_event = {
	id = health.124
	desc = EVTDESChealth124
	picture = GFX_evt_torture
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_bi_yearly_pulse, health_voices list

	only_playable = yes
	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no

	trigger = {
		health_daemon_voice_trigger = yes
		any_courtier = {
			prisoner = yes
			host = { character = ROOT }
			NOT = { has_character_modifier = house_arrest }
		}
	}

	option = { # Bring the knives
		name = EVTOPTAhealth124
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				prisoner = yes
				host = { character = ROOT }
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = house_arrest }
			}
			health = -1
		}
	}
}

#######################################
# Lunatic laws 150-158
# A mad independent ruler passes an absurd law. The ruler hears how it is
# carried out (155-158); every vassal receives it (151-154) and thinks less
# of the ruler.
#######################################

# health.150 A new law
# A lunatic independent ruler has an idea for a law. Each choice tells the
# ruler and every vassal. About once in 35 years.
# Scope: ROOT is the lunatic ruler.
character_event = {
	id = health.150
	desc = EVTDESChealth150
	picture = GFX_evt_lunatic
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_five_year_pulse, health_madness list

	only_playable = yes
	only_independent = yes
	min_age = 16
	prisoner = no

	trigger = {
		is_lunatic_trigger = yes
	}

	option = { # Turnips are sacred
		name = EVTOPTAhealth150
		tooltip_info = lunatic_3
		narrative_event = { id = health.155 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPhealth155 }
		hidden_tooltip = { any_vassal = { narrative_event = { id = health.151 } } }
	}
	option = { # Breeches are forbidden
		name = EVTOPTBhealth150
		tooltip_info = lunatic_3
		narrative_event = { id = health.156 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPhealth156 }
		hidden_tooltip = { any_vassal = { narrative_event = { id = health.152 } } }
	}
	option = { # Every subject digs a hole
		name = EVTOPTChealth150
		tooltip_info = lunatic_3
		narrative_event = { id = health.157 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPhealth157 }
		hidden_tooltip = { any_vassal = { narrative_event = { id = health.153 } } }
	}
	option = { # Strike the neighbour
		name = EVTOPTDhealth150
		tooltip_info = lunatic_3
		narrative_event = { id = health.158 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPhealth158 }
		hidden_tooltip = { any_vassal = { narrative_event = { id = health.154 } } }
	}
}

	}

# health.161 Howling at the moons
# A player lunatic howls at the moons from the battlements. When Morrslieb is
# full (beastmen.85), the green moon answers. About once in 35 years.
# Scope: ROOT is the lunatic.
character_event = {
	id = health.161
	desc = { text = EVTDESChealth161 trigger = { NOT = { has_global_flag = morrslieb_full } } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESChealth161b trigger = { has_global_flag = morrslieb_full } }
	picture = GFX_evt_lunatic
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_five_year_pulse, health_madness list

	ai = no
	min_age = 16
	prisoner = no

	trigger = {
		is_lunatic_trigger = yes
	}

	option = { # Awoooo
		name = EVTOPTAhealth161
		tooltip_info = lunatic_3
		prestige = -20
		if = {
			limit = { has_global_flag = morrslieb_full }
			change_variable = { which = "lunatic_counter" value = 1 }
			lunacy_counter_check_effect = yes
		}
	}
}

# health.162 The horse chancellor
# A lunatic ruler dismisses the chancellor and gives the seat to a horse. The
# chancellor is told (health.163). Only without the Zeus DLC, whose
# ze_lunatic.txt has a longer horse chancellor story. About once in 35 years.
# Scope: ROOT is the lunatic ruler.
character_event = {
	id = health.162
	desc = EVTDESChealth162
	picture = GFX_evt_courier
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_five_year_pulse, health_madness list

	only_playable = yes
	min_age = 16
	prisoner = no
	capable_only = yes
	lacks_dlc = "Zeus"

	trigger = {
		is_lunatic_trigger = yes
		NOT = { has_game_rule = { name = supernatural_events value = off } }
		any_courtier = { has_job_title = job_chancellor }
	}

	option = { # A fine horse
		name = EVTOPTAhealth162
		tooltip_info = lunatic_3
		job_chancellor = {
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_fired_from_council who = ROOT years = 5 }
			hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = health.163 } }
			remove_title = job_chancellor
		}
	}
}

# health.163 Replaced by a horse
# The dismissed chancellor learns who took the seat. Also fired by
# ze_lunatic.txt and WHG_waaagh_events.txt.
# Scope: ROOT is the former chancellor, FROM the lunatic ruler.
character_event = {
	id = health.163
	desc = EVTDESChealth163
	picture = GFX_evt_courier
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	is_triggered_only = yes # health.162, ze_lunatic.txt, WHG_waaagh_events.txt

	option = { # A horse!
		name = EVTOPTAhealth163
		prestige = -20
		custom_tooltip = { text = horse_chancellor }
	}
}

#######################################
# Drowned dreams 170-178
# A raving lunatic of the northern faiths of the Old World is drawn by dreams
# to a fishing village on the Sea of Claws, where the villagers pray to
# something beneath the waves. Once in a lifetime (flag
# health_drowned_dreams). Players only.
#######################################

# health.170 Rats in the walls
# The lunatic hears scratching in the walls and talk of the Horned Rat, though
# everyone knows the ratmen are a tale. The dreams begin.
# Scope: ROOT is the lunatic.
character_event = {
	id = health.170
	desc = EVTDESChealth170
	picture = GFX_evt_recovery
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_five_year_pulse, health_drowned_dreams list

	ai = no
	only_playable = yes
	min_age = 16
	prisoner = no

	trigger = {
		health_drowned_dreams_trigger = yes
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		days = 1
		modifier = { factor = 0.01 NOT = { trait = lunatic_3 } } # Raving lunatics only, almost always
	}

	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = health_drowned_dreams
	}

	option = { # Follow the dreams
		name = EVTOPTAhealth170
		if = {
			limit = { is_lunatic_trigger = no }
			add_trait = lunatic_1
			set_variable = { which = "lunatic_counter" value = 5 }
		}
		character_event = { id = health.174 days = 14 tooltip = EVTTOOLTIPhealth174 }
	}
	option = { # Call the ratcatcher
		name = EVTOPTBhealth170
		trigger = { NOR = { trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 } }
	}
}

# health.178 Waking on the shore
# The lunatic wakes on the beach with a great tooth in hand. Either it was a
# fever dream, and the lunatic gains prestige and learning (or the scholar
# trait), or they slew the thing and keep the tooth (health_drowned_tooth)
# and the killed_an_old_one modifier.
# Scope: ROOT is the lunatic.
character_event = {
	id = health.178
	desc = EVTDESChealth178
	picture = GFX_evt_recovery
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	is_triggered_only = yes # health.177
	hide_from = yes

	option = { # A fever dream
		name = EVTOPTAhealth178
		prestige = 100
		if = { limit = { lifestyle_traits = 1 } change_learning = 1 }
		else = { add_trait = scholar }
	}
	option = { # I slew it
		name = EVTOPTBhealth178

#######################################
# Mittens the spymaster 180-185
# Only with the silly events game rule on (unrestricted for the AI).
#######################################

# health.180 Mittens
# A lunatic ruler with a pet cat brings it to the council. The spymaster
# blames it for a sneeze. Defend Mittens and the story goes on.
# Scope: ROOT is the lunatic ruler.
character_event = {
	id = health.180
	desc = { trigger = { has_dlc = "Reapers" } picture = GFX_evt_cat text = EVTDESChealth180 }
	desc = {
		trigger = { NOT = { has_dlc = "Reapers" } }
		picture = GFX_evt_council
		text = EVTDESChealth180
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_cat
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_yearly_pulse, pet events list (00_on_actions.txt)

	only_playable = yes
	min_age = 16
	prisoner = no
	capable_only = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = { ai = no has_game_rule = { name = silly_events value = unrestricted } }
		NOR = {
			has_game_rule = { name = silly_events value = off }
			has_character_flag = cat_spymaster_flag
		}
		is_lunatic_trigger = yes
		any_courtier = { has_job_title = job_spymaster ai = yes }
		has_character_modifier = pet_cat
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		days = 1
	}

	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = cat_spymaster_flag
		random_courtier = {
			limit = { has_job_title = job_spymaster ai = yes }
			save_event_target_as = show
		}
	}

	option = { # How dare you blame Mittens?
		name = EVTOPTAhealth180
		custom_tooltip = { text = cat_start }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = health.181 } }
	}
	option = { # The council is no place for a cat
		name = EVTOPTBhealth180
		job_spymaster = { opinion = { modifier = opinion_no_cat who = ROOT years = 100 } }
	}
}

# health.181 Sleepless Mittens
# Mittens wakes the ruler at night and leads them down a dark corridor.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = health.181
	desc = EVTDESChealth181
	picture = GFX_evt_kitten_shadow
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	is_triggered_only = yes # health.180
	hide_from = yes

	trigger = {
		has_character_modifier = pet_cat
	}

	option = { # Follow Mittens
		name = EVTOPTAhealth181
		custom_tooltip = { text = cat_corridor }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = health.182 } }
	}
	option = { # Go to the kitchen
		name = EVTOPTBhealth181
		custom_tooltip = { text = cat_kitchen }
	}
}

# health.182 Mittens the spymaster
# The spymaster lies dead, Mittens on their chest. The ruler appoints the cat
# spymaster. The council hears of it (health.183).
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = health.182
	desc = EVTDESChealth182
	picture = GFX_evt_kitten_shadow
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	is_triggered_only = yes # health.181

	trigger = {
		has_character_modifier = pet_cat
	}

	option = { # Clever kitty
		name = EVTOPTAhealth182
		tooltip_info_custom = lunatic
		job_spymaster = { death = { death_reason = death_murder_unknown } }
		create_character = {
			name = "Mittens"
			age = 16
			religion = ROOT
			culture = cat
			race = cat
			dynasty = NONE
			trait = cat
			trait = imbecile
			trait = deceitful
			trait = fair
			trait = wroth
			trait = proud
		}
		new_character = {
			give_job_title = job_spymaster
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_my_servant who = ROOT years = 100 }
			reverse_opinion = { modifier = opinion_my_kitten who = ROOT years = 100 }
			character_event = { id = health.183 }
		}
		hidden_tooltip = { remove_character_modifier = pet_cat }
	}
}

# health.183 Mittens takes the seat
# Hidden. The other councillors hear of their new colleague (health.184). An
# immortal ruler may share their immortality with the cat (health.185).
# Scope: ROOT is Mittens, FROM the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = health.183
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes # health.182

	immediate = {
		FROM = {
			any_courtier = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_job_title = job_chancellor
						has_job_title = job_marshal
						has_job_title = job_treasurer
						has_job_title = job_spiritual
					}
				}
				character_event = { id = health.184 }
			}
			if = { limit = { trait = immortal } character_event = { id = health.185 days = 10 } }
		}
	}
}

# health.184 A cat on the council
# A councillor despairs of the new spymaster.
# Scope: ROOT is a councillor, FROM Mittens, FROMFROM the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = health.184
	desc = EVTDESChealth184
	picture = GFX_evt_kitten_shadow
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	is_triggered_only = yes # health.183
	hide_new = yes

	option = { # A catastrophe
		name = EVTOPTAhealth184
		opinion = { modifier = opinion_cat_spymaster who = FROMFROM years = 100 }
	}
}

# health.185 Immortal Mittens
# An immortal lunatic ruler can give Mittens immortality too.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler, FROM Mittens.
character_event = {
	id = health.185
	desc = EVTDESChealth185
	picture = GFX_evt_kitten_shadow
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	is_triggered_only = yes # health.183

	trigger = {
		is_lunatic_trigger = yes
		trait = immortal
	}

	option = { # Share my blood
		name = EVTOPTAhealth185
		FROM = { add_trait = immortal }
	}
	option = { # This power is mine alone
		name = EVTOPTBhealth185
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Lunacy events
# Namespace: lunacy (was "slunacy" before the 2026 events refactor)
# Faction: Generic
#
# A lunatic (lunatic_1 twitchy, lunatic_2 maniac, lunatic_3 raving) or the
# wearer of a madness artifact slowly loses their mind. Maniacs and raving
# lunatics catch one of nine madnesses (character modifiers), each with its own
# follow-up events. Old vampires go mad with age and catch vampire madnesses.
# Each madness event feeds the lunatic_counter variable through mutate.5.
#
# Umbrella and twitchy events   lunacy.1 - lunacy.9
# Madness onsets                lunacy.10 - lunacy.15
# Bloodthirsty                  lunacy.16 - lunacy.21
# Blind Virtue                  lunacy.22 - lunacy.24
# Fake Mage                     lunacy.25 - lunacy.26
# Despairing Laughter           lunacy.27 - lunacy.29
# Unfeeling                     lunacy.30 - lunacy.32, lunacy.35 - lunacy.42
# God Delusion                  lunacy.33 - lunacy.34
# Vampire ageing and umbrella   lunacy.50 - lunacy.51
# Vampire madness onsets        lunacy.52 - lunacy.54
# Everchild Syndrome            lunacy.60 - lunacy.67
# Exquisite Agonies             lunacy.70 - lunacy.82
# Heart of Melancholy           lunacy.85 - lunacy.88
# Recovery                      lunacy.90
#
# Fired from common/on_actions/wh_on_actions.txt: the bi-yearly list lunacy
# (lunacy.1) and lunacy.51 every two years, the five-year lists
# lunacy_<madness> and the decade lists lunacy_vampire_aging, lunacy_everchild
# and lunacy_hibernation.
# Scripted triggers: common/scripted_triggers/wh_lunacy_triggers.txt
# Scripted effects: common/scripted_effects/wh_lunacy_effects.txt
# Artifacts: common/artifacts/22_artifacts_lunacy.txt
# Loc: localisation/00_WHG_health_events.csv
# Full list with callers: docs/events/event_database.csv
###############################################################################

namespace = lunacy

###############################################################################
# Umbrella and twitchy events
###############################################################################

# lunacy.1 Lunacy umbrella
# Every two years a mortal lunatic or madness artifact wearer may suffer a
# lunacy event: a twitchy quirk, or for maniacs and raving lunatics without a
# madness the onset of one. Doom, Morrslieb, mutations, Chaos cults and cursed
# lands make it more likely; a madness artifact alone makes it less likely.
# Scope: ROOT is the lunatic.
character_event = {
	id = lunacy.1
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_bi_yearly_pulse, random list lunacy

	only_playable = yes

	trigger = {
		lunacy_can_go_mad_trigger = yes
		is_vampire_trigger = no
		OR = {
			trait = lunatic_1
			trait = lunatic_2
			trait = lunatic_3
			lunacy_madness_artifact_trigger = yes
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1
		# Morrslieb and doom
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 has_global_flag = morrslieb_full }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 is_doom_very_low_trigger = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 1.2 is_doom_low_trigger = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 1.5 is_doom_medium_trigger = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 1.75 is_doom_high_trigger = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 2 is_doom_very_high_trigger = yes }
		# A madness artifact alone is not true lunacy, and a healthy body resists it
		modifier = { factor = 0.6 NOR = { trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.9 lunacy_madness_artifact_trigger = yes health = 6 }
		modifier = { factor = 0.9 lunacy_madness_artifact_trigger = yes health = 8 }
		modifier = { factor = 0.9 lunacy_madness_artifact_trigger = yes health = 10 }
		# Race and body
		modifier = { factor = 0.9 kislev_northern_blood_trigger = yes } # Northern blood (WHG_bulwark)
		modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = creature_dwarf }
		modifier = {
			factor = 0.75
			OR = {
				trait = creature_orc
				trait = creature_snotling
				trait = creature_goblin
				trait = creature_hobgoblin
			}
		}
		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = creature_fimir }
		modifier = { factor = 2 trait = creature_chaos_dwarf }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = creature_halfling }
		modifier = { factor = 1.5 has_minor_mutation_trigger = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 2 has_major_mutation_trigger = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 3 has_massive_mutation_trigger = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = addict }
		modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = lore_tzeentch }
		# The unschooled mind has no defence against the Winds of Magic
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.25
			NOR = {
				trait = magic_potential_1
				trait = magic_potential_2
				trait = magic_potential_3
				trait = magic_potential_4
			}
		}
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 has_magic_power_trigger = no }
		modifier = { factor = 2 has_character_flag = forsaken_flag }
		# Service of the Dark Gods
		modifier = { factor = 1.5 religion_group = chaos_gods_group }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 society_member_of = cult_of_tzeentch }
		modifier = { factor = 1.5 society_member_of = cult_of_tzeentch2 }
		modifier = { factor = 1.75 society_member_of = cult_of_tzeentch3 }
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.25
			OR = {
				society_member_of = cult_of_khorne
				society_member_of = cult_of_slaanesh
				society_member_of = cult_of_nurgle
				society_member_of = cult_of_undivided
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.25
			OR = {
				society_member_of = cult_of_khorne2
				society_member_of = cult_of_slaanesh2
				society_member_of = cult_of_nurgle2
				society_member_of = cult_of_undivided2
			}
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.25
			OR = {
				society_member_of = cult_of_khorne3
				society_member_of = cult_of_slaanesh3
				society_member_of = cult_of_nurgle3
				society_member_of = cult_of_undivided3
			}
		}
		# Cursed lands
		modifier = { factor = 1.5 location = { empire = { title = e_chaos_wastes } } }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 location = { kingdom = { title = k_sylvania } } }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 location = { kingdom = { title = k_mousillon } } }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 location = { duchy = { title = d_geisterforst } } }
		modifier = { factor = 1.5 location = { has_province_modifier = warpdust_traces } }
		modifier = { factor = 0.5 ai = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			20 = { } # Nothing this time
			10 = { # Twitchy: whispers and shadows
				modifier = { factor = 0 lunacy_twitchy_trigger = no }
				modifier = { factor = 0 trait = paranoid }
				character_event = { id = lunacy.2 }
			}
			10 = { # Twitchy: unmoved by suffering
				modifier = { factor = 0 lunacy_twitchy_trigger = no }
				modifier = { factor = 0 trait = cruel }
				character_event = { id = lunacy.3 }
			}
			10 = { # Twitchy: too stressed
				modifier = { factor = 0 lunacy_twitchy_trigger = no }
				modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { trait = stressed } }
				character_event = { id = lunacy.4 }
			}
			5 = { # Twitchy mage: dangerous experiment
				modifier = { factor = 0 lunacy_twitchy_trigger = no }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_magic_power_trigger = no }
				character_event = { id = lunacy.5 }
			}
			5 = { # Twitchy: the drunkard feels poisoned
				modifier = { factor = 0 lunacy_twitchy_trigger = no }
				modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { trait = drunkard } }
				character_event = { id = lunacy.9 }
			}
			10 = { # Maniac: Blind Virtue
				modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { trait = lunatic_2 } }
				modifier = { factor = 0 lunacy_has_madness_trigger = yes }
				character_event = { id = lunacy.10 }
			}
			10 = { # Maniac: Fake Mage
				modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { trait = lunatic_2 } }
				modifier = { factor = 0 lunacy_has_madness_trigger = yes }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_magic_power_trigger = yes }
				character_event = { id = lunacy.11 }
			}
			10 = { # Maniac: Despairing Laughter
				modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { trait = lunatic_2 } }
				modifier = { factor = 0 lunacy_has_madness_trigger = yes }
				character_event = { id = lunacy.12 }
			}
			10 = { # Maniac: Unfeeling
				modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { trait = lunatic_2 } }
				modifier = { factor = 0 lunacy_has_madness_trigger = yes }
				character_event = { id = lunacy.13 }
			}
			10 = { # Raving: Bloodthirsty
				modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { trait = lunatic_3 } }
				modifier = { factor = 0 lunacy_has_madness_trigger = yes }
				character_event = { id = lunacy.14 }
			}
			10 = { # Raving: God Delusion
				modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { trait = lunatic_3 } }
				modifier = { factor = 0 lunacy_has_madness_trigger = yes }
				modifier = { factor = 0 is_ruler = no }
				character_event = { id = lunacy.15 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# lunacy.2 Whispers and shadows
# A twitchy lunatic hears whispers behind their back and may turn paranoid.
# Scope: ROOT is the lunatic.
character_event = {
	id = lunacy.2
	desc = EVTDESClunacy2
	picture = GFX_evt_whispers
	is_triggered_only = yes # lunacy.1, lunacy.51

	trigger = {
		lunacy_twitchy_trigger = yes
		NOT = { trait = paranoid }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlunacy2
		random = { chance = 50 add_trait = paranoid }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = mutate.5 } } # Lunacy counter
	}
}

# lunacy.3 Unmoved by suffering
# A twitchy lunatic stops caring about the misery of others and may turn cruel.
# Scope: ROOT is the lunatic.
character_event = {
	id = lunacy.3
	desc = EVTDESClunacy3
	picture = GFX_evt_lunatic
	is_triggered_only = yes # lunacy.1, lunacy.51

	trigger = {
		lunacy_twitchy_trigger = yes
		NOT = { trait = cruel }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlunacy3
		random = { chance = 50 add_trait = cruel }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = mutate.5 } } # Lunacy counter
	}
}

# lunacy.4 Too stressed
# A stressed twitchy lunatic reaches for herbs to calm down and may trade the
# stress for an addiction.
# Scope: ROOT is the lunatic.
character_event = {
	id = lunacy.4
	desc = EVTDESClunacy4
	picture = GFX_evt_lunatic
	is_triggered_only = yes # lunacy.1

	trigger = {
		lunacy_twitchy_trigger = yes
		trait = stressed
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlunacy4
		random = { chance = 50 remove_trait = stressed add_trait = addict }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = mutate.5 } } # Lunacy counter
	}
}

# lunacy.5 Dangerous experiment
# A twitchy mage tries a reckless spell. Patience and greater power make
# success (lunacy.6) likelier than failure (lunacy.7) or disaster (lunacy.8).
# Scope: ROOT is the mage.
character_event = {
	id = lunacy.5
	desc = EVTDESClunacy5
	picture = GFX_evt_lunatic
	is_triggered_only = yes # lunacy.1, lunacy.51

	trigger = {
		lunacy_twitchy_trigger = yes
		has_magic_power_trigger = yes
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlunacy5
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				33 = {
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = patient }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 has_character_modifier = magic_power_4 }
					modifier = { factor = 1.5 has_character_modifier = magic_power_3 }
					character_event = { id = lunacy.6 }
				}
				34 = { character_event = { id = lunacy.7 } }
				33 = { character_event = { id = lunacy.8 } }
			}
		}
	}
}

# lunacy.6 The experiment succeeds
# The mage gains learning, and may keep their feverish notes: a library item
# that raises learning but carries the madness to whoever reads it.
# Scope: ROOT is the mage.
character_event = {
	id = lunacy.6
	desc = EVTDESClunacy6
	picture = GFX_evt_lunatic
	is_triggered_only = yes # lunacy.5

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlunacy6
		change_learning = 1
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_artifact = lunacy_feverish_notes } }
			random = { chance = 25 add_artifact = lunacy_feverish_notes }
		}
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = mutate.5 } } # Lunacy counter
	}
}

# lunacy.7 The experiment fails
# A wasted night drives the mage a little further into madness.
# Scope: ROOT is the mage.
character_event = {
	id = lunacy.7
	desc = EVTDESClunacy7
	picture = GFX_evt_lunatic
	is_triggered_only = yes # lunacy.5

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlunacy7
		change_variable = { which = "lunatic_counter" value = 1 }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = mutate.5 } } # Lunacy counter
	}
}

# lunacy.8 The experiment goes up in flames
# The fire catches the mage: disfigured, or wounded or maimed if already
# disfigured. Drives them further into madness.
# Scope: ROOT is the mage.
character_event = {
	id = lunacy.8
	desc = EVTDESClunacy8
	picture = GFX_evt_lunatic
	is_triggered_only = yes # lunacy.5

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlunacy8
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = disfigured } } add_trait = disfigured }
		else_if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = wounded } } add_trait = wounded }
		else = { remove_trait = wounded add_trait = maimed }
		change_variable = { which = "lunatic_counter" value = 1 }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = mutate.5 } } # Lunacy counter
	}
}

# lunacy.9 The drunkard feels poisoned
# A twitchy drunkard decides drink is poison and gives it up.
# Scope: ROOT is the lunatic.
character_event = {
	id = lunacy.9
	desc = EVTDESClunacy9
	picture = GFX_evt_lunatic
	is_triggered_only = yes # lunacy.1

	trigger = {
		lunacy_twitchy_trigger = yes
		trait = drunkard
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlunacy9
		remove_trait = drunkard
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = mutate.5 } } # Lunacy counter
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Madness onsets
# Each costs piety under a faith that frowns on madness (more under the god
# who hates that madness most), shocks the court of such a faith, and pleases
# the god who loves it.
###############################################################################

# lunacy.10 Blind Virtue
# A maniac decides there is no evil in the world. Nurgle, god of despair,
# scorns such hope.
# Scope: ROOT is the maniac.
character_event = {
	id = lunacy.10
	desc = EVTDESClunacy10
	picture = GFX_evt_lunatic
	is_triggered_only = yes # lunacy.1

	trigger = {
		lunacy_maniac_trigger = yes
		lunacy_has_madness_trigger = no
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlunacy10
		if = { limit = { religion = nurgle } piety = -200 }
		else_if = { limit = { lunacy_sane_faith_trigger = yes } piety = -100 }
		lunacy_court_shocked_effect = yes
		change_variable = { which = "lunatic_counter" value = 3 }
		add_character_modifier = { name = blind_virtue duration = -1 }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = mutate.5 } } # Lunacy counter
	}
}

# lunacy.11 Fake Mage
# A maniac without magic believes the Winds flow through them. Khorne, who
# loathes sorcery, despises it; Tzeentch is amused.
# Scope: ROOT is the maniac.
character_event = {
	id = lunacy.11
	desc = EVTDESClunacy11
	picture = GFX_evt_lunatic
	is_triggered_only = yes # lunacy.1

	trigger = {
		lunacy_maniac_trigger = yes
		lunacy_has_madness_trigger = no
		has_magic_power_trigger = no
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlunacy11
		if = { limit = { religion = khorne } piety = -200 }
		else_if = { limit = { religion = tzeentch } piety = 100 }
		else_if = { limit = { lunacy_sane_faith_trigger = yes } piety = -100 }
		lunacy_court_shocked_effect = yes
		change_variable = { which = "lunatic_counter" value = 3 }
		add_character_modifier = { name = fake_mage duration = -1 }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = mutate.5 } } # Lunacy counter
	}
}

# lunacy.12 Despairing Laughter
# A maniac sees the whole world as a cruel joke and laughs at every misery.
# Scope: ROOT is the maniac.
character_event = {
	id = lunacy.12
	desc = EVTDESClunacy12
	picture = GFX_evt_lunatic
	is_triggered_only = yes # lunacy.1

	trigger = {
		lunacy_maniac_trigger = yes
		lunacy_has_madness_trigger = no

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlunacy12
		if = { limit = { lunacy_sane_faith_trigger = yes } piety = -100 }

		add_character_modifier = { name = despairing_laughter duration = -1 }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = mutate.5 } } # Lunacy counter
	}
}

# lunacy.13 Unfeeling
# A maniac stops feeling anything at all. Slaanesh, who craves sensation,
# despises the numbness.
# Scope: ROOT is the maniac.
character_event = {
	id = lunacy.13
	desc = EVTDESClunacy13
	picture = GFX_evt_lunatic

		lunacy_maniac_trigger = yes
		lunacy_has_madness_trigger = no

		else_if = { limit = { lunacy_sane_faith_trigger = yes } piety = -100 }
		lunacy_court_shocked_effect = yes
		change_variable = { which = "lunatic_counter" value = 3 }
		add_character_modifier = { name = unfeeling duration = -1 }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = mutate.5 } } # Lunacy counter
	}
}

# lunacy.14 Bloodthirsty
# A raving lunatic (or a maniac vampire) craves the moment of another's death.
# The Blood God approves.
# Scope: ROOT is the lunatic.
character_event = {
	id = lunacy.14
	desc = EVTDESClunacy14
	picture = GFX_evt_lunatic
	is_triggered_only = yes # lunacy.1, lunacy.51

	trigger = {
		OR = { trait = lunatic_2 lunacy_raving_trigger = yes }
		lunacy_has_madness_trigger = no
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlunacy14
		if = { limit = { religion = khorne } piety = 100 }
		else_if = { limit = { lunacy_sane_faith_trigger = yes } piety = -100 }
		lunacy_court_shocked_effect = yes
		change_variable = { which = "lunatic_counter" value = 4 }
		add_character_modifier = { name = bloodthirsty duration = -1 }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = mutate.5 } } # Lunacy counter
	}
}

# lunacy.15 God Delusion
# A raving lunatic ruler (or a maniac vampire ruler) comes to believe they are
# a god. Blasphemy under any faith that frowns on madness.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = lunacy.15
	desc = EVTDESClunacy15
	picture = GFX_evt_lunatic
	is_triggered_only = yes # lunacy.1, lunacy.51

	only_rulers = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = { trait = lunatic_2 lunacy_raving_trigger = yes }
		lunacy_has_madness_trigger = no
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlunacy15
		if = { limit = { lunacy_sane_faith_trigger = yes } piety = -200 }
		lunacy_court_shocked_effect = yes
		change_variable = { which = "lunatic_counter" value = 4 }
		add_character_modifier = { name = god_delusion duration = -1 }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = mutate.5 } } # Lunacy counter
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Bloodthirsty
###############################################################################

# lunacy.16 The bloodthirsty lunatic kills
# A bloodthirsty raving lunatic murders a courtier (lunacy_murder_victim_trigger)
# of the court they live in.
# Scope: ROOT is the murderer.
character_event = {
	id = lunacy.16
	desc = EVTDESClunacy16
	picture = GFX_evt_lunatic
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_five_year_pulse, random list lunacy_bloodthirsty

	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no

	trigger = {
		has_character_modifier = bloodthirsty
		lunacy_raving_trigger = yes
		lunacy_can_act_trigger = yes
		NOR = { trait = studying_magic trait = crafting_magic_item }
		host = { any_courtier = { lunacy_murder_victim_trigger = yes } }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlunacy16
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = mutate.5 } } # Lunacy counter
		host = {
			random_courtier = {
				limit = { lunacy_murder_victim_trigger = yes }
				character_event = { id = lunacy.17 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# lunacy.17 The victim dies
# The murder victim dies unless they cannot die. Their close kin, spouse and
# liege learn who did it (lunacy.18) and hate the murderer.
# Scope: ROOT is the victim, FROM the murderer.
character_event = {
	id = lunacy.17
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes # lunacy.16

	immediate = {
		if = { limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes } antideath_effect = yes }
		else = {
			any_dynasty_member = {
				limit = { is_close_relative = ROOT NOT = { character = FROM } }
				opinion = { modifier = opinion_murderous_lunatic who = FROM years = 100 }
				character_event = { id = lunacy.18 }
			}
			any_spouse = {
				limit = { NOT = { character = FROM } }
				opinion = { modifier = opinion_murderous_lunatic who = FROM years = 100 }
				character_event = { id = lunacy.18 }
			}
			liege = {
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { character = FROM } }
					opinion = { modifier = opinion_murderous_lunatic who = FROM years = 10 }
					character_event = { id = lunacy.18 }
				}
			}
			death = { death_reason = death_murder killer = FROM }
		}
	}
}

# lunacy.18 Murder notice
# A relative, spouse or liege of the victim learns of the senseless murder.
# Scope: ROOT is the one told, FROM the victim, FROMFROM the murderer.
character_event = {
	id = lunacy.18
	desc = EVTDESClunacy18
	picture = GFX_evt_lunatic
	is_triggered_only = yes # lunacy.17

	trigger = {
		NOT = { character = FROMFROM }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlunacy18
	}
}

# lunacy.19 Killing spree
# A bloodthirsty raving ruler stalks their own lands for a week. One demesne
# province takes the bloodthirsty_rampage modifier for 800 days.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = lunacy.19
	desc = EVTDESClunacy19
	picture = GFX_evt_lunatic
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_five_year_pulse, random list lunacy_bloodthirsty

	only_rulers = yes
	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no

	trigger = {
		has_character_modifier = bloodthirsty
		lunacy_raving_trigger = yes
		lunacy_can_act_trigger = yes
		NOR = { trait = studying_magic trait = crafting_magic_item }
		any_demesne_province = { NOT = { has_province_modifier = bloodthirsty_rampage } }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlunacy19
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = mutate.5 } } # Lunacy counter
		random_demesne_province = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_province_modifier = bloodthirsty_rampage } }
			add_province_modifier = { name = bloodthirsty_rampage duration = 800 }
		}
	}
}

# lunacy.20 The mage blows up a building
# A bloodthirsty raving mage ruler destroys a building where they stand. The
# province's owner hears of it (lunacy.21).
# Scope: ROOT is the mage.
character_event = {
	id = lunacy.20
	desc = EVTDESClunacy20
	picture = GFX_evt_lunatic
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_five_year_pulse, random list lunacy_bloodthirsty

	only_rulers = yes
	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no

	trigger = {
		has_character_modifier = bloodthirsty
		lunacy_raving_trigger = yes
		lunacy_can_act_trigger = yes
		has_magic_power_trigger = yes
		NOR = { trait = studying_magic trait = crafting_magic_item }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlunacy20
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = mutate.5 } } # Lunacy counter
		location = {
			destroy_random_building = yes
			owner = { character_event = { id = lunacy.21 } }
		}
	}
}

# lunacy.21 Destruction notice
# The owner of the province learns that the mad mage blew up a building.
# Scope: ROOT is the owner, FROM the mage.
character_event = {
	id = lunacy.21
	desc = EVTDESClunacy21
	picture = GFX_evt_lunatic
	is_triggered_only = yes # lunacy.20

	trigger = {
		NOT = { character = FROM }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlunacy21
		opinion = { modifier = opinion_murderous_lunatic who = FROM years = 10 }
	}
}

# lunacy.25 The fake mage becomes real
# A Fake Mage who has since gained true magic power sees the delusion fall
# away (nine times in ten per five years): the madness ends and the lunatic counter drops by 3.
# Scope: ROOT is the mage.
character_event = {
	id = lunacy.25
	desc = EVTDESClunacy25
	picture = GFX_evt_lunatic
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_five_year_pulse, random list lunacy_fake_mage_cured

	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_character_modifier = fake_mage
		has_magic_power_trigger = yes
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlunacy25
		remove_character_modifier = fake_mage
		change_variable = { which = "lunatic_counter" value = -3 }
		lunacy_counter_settle_effect = yes
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Despairing Laughter
###############################################################################

# lunacy.27 Mockery
# A maniac with Despairing Laughter spends a day mocking a courtier for their
# weakness (lunacy_mock_target_trigger). The victim is told (lunacy.28).
# Scope: ROOT is the maniac.
character_event = {
	id = lunacy.27
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_five_year_pulse, random list lunacy_laughter

	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_character_modifier = despairing_laughter
		lunacy_maniac_trigger = yes
		lunacy_can_act_trigger = yes
		NOT = { trait = wounded }
		host = { any_courtier = { lunacy_mock_target_trigger = yes } }
	}

	immediate = {
		character_event = { id = mutate.5 } # Lunacy counter
		host = {
			random_courtier = {
				limit = { lunacy_mock_target_trigger = yes }
				character_event = { id = lunacy.28 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# lunacy.28 Mocked
# The courtier mocked by the maniac takes offence and may sink into
# depression. The maniac sees the result (lunacy.29).
# Scope: ROOT is the victim, FROM the maniac.
character_event = {
	id = lunacy.28
	desc = { text = EVTDESClunacy28 trigger = { trait = infirm } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESClunacy28b trigger = { OR = { trait = maimed trait = wounded } } }
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESClunacy28c
		trigger = { OR = { trait = disfigured trait = burnt_by_asuryan } }
	}
	desc = { text = EVTDESClunacy28d trigger = { trait = is_fat } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESClunacy28e trigger = { trait = is_malnourished } }
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESClunacy28f
		trigger = { OR = { trait = leper trait = pneumonic trait = ill trait = syphilitic } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESClunacy28g
		trigger = { OR = { trait = hard_pregnancy trait = troubled_pregnancy } }
	}
	desc = { text = EVTDESClunacy28h trigger = { is_mutated_trigger = yes } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESClunacy28i trigger = { trait = lunatic_3 } }
	picture = GFX_evt_lunatic
	is_triggered_only = yes # lunacy.27

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlunacy28
		opinion = { modifier = opinion_evil_laughter who = FROM years = 5 }
		random = { chance = 10 add_trait = depressed }
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = lunacy.29 } } }
	}
}

# lunacy.29 Laughing at misery
# The maniac laughs at the courtier's misery and may turn cruel.
# Scope: ROOT is the maniac, FROM the victim.
character_event = {
	id = lunacy.29
	desc = { text = EVTDESClunacy29 trigger = { FROM = { trait = infirm } } }
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESClunacy29b
		trigger = { FROM = { OR = { trait = maimed trait = wounded } } }
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESClunacy29c
		trigger = { FROM = { OR = { trait = disfigured trait = burnt_by_asuryan } } }
	}
	desc = { text = EVTDESClunacy29d trigger = { FROM = { trait = is_fat } } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESClunacy29e trigger = { FROM = { trait = is_malnourished } } }
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESClunacy29f
		trigger = {
			FROM = { OR = { trait = leper trait = pneumonic trait = ill trait = syphilitic } }
		}
	}
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESClunacy29g
		trigger = { FROM = { OR = { trait = hard_pregnancy trait = troubled_pregnancy } } }
	}
	desc = { text = EVTDESClunacy29h trigger = { FROM = { is_mutated_trigger = yes } } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESClunacy29i trigger = { FROM = { trait = lunatic_3 } } }
	picture = GFX_evt_lunatic
	is_triggered_only = yes # lunacy.28

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlunacy29
		random = { chance = 10 add_trait = cruel }
	}
}

	desc = EVTDESClunacy31

	capable_only = yes

		lunacy_can_act_trigger = yes
		trait = paranoid
	}

# lunacy.32 Cutting to feel
# An Unfeeling maniac cuts themselves to feel something: wounded, then maybe
# maimed, and a maimed one may end up incapable.
# Scope: ROOT is the maniac.
character_event = {
	id = lunacy.32
	desc = EVTDESClunacy32
	picture = GFX_evt_lunatic
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_five_year_pulse, random list lunacy_unfeeling

	min_age = 12
	capable_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_character_modifier = unfeeling
		lunacy_maniac_trigger = yes
		lunacy_can_act_trigger = yes
		NOT = { trait = craven }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlunacy32
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = mutate.5 } } # Lunacy counter
		if = {
			limit = { trait = maimed }
			random = { chance = 15 remove_trait = maimed add_trait = incapable }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { OR = { trait = scarred trait = wounded } }
			random = { chance = 30 add_trait = maimed }
		}
		else = { add_trait = wounded }
	}
}

###############################################################################
# God Delusion
###############################################################################

# lunacy.33 Worship me
# A ruler with the God Delusion demands worship: prestige, lost piety, and
# perhaps a statue of the living god for their court. Immortals do this more
# often.
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = lunacy.33
	desc = { text = EVTDESClunacy33 trigger = { is_female = no } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESClunacy33b trigger = { is_female = yes } }
	picture = GFX_evt_lunatic
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_five_year_pulse, random list lunacy_god_delusion

	only_rulers = yes
	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no

	trigger = {
		has_character_modifier = god_delusion
		lunacy_raving_trigger = yes
		lunacy_can_act_trigger = yes
		NOR = { trait = studying_magic trait = crafting_magic_item }
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1
		modifier = { factor = 1.33 trait = immortal }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlunacy33
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = mutate.5 } } # Lunacy counter
		prestige = 350
		piety = -400
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_artifact = lunacy_living_god_idol } }
			random = { chance = 50 add_artifact = lunacy_living_god_idol }
		}
	}
}

# lunacy.34 Lord and Master
# A ruler with the God Delusion who is not yet of Chaos decides they need no
# other god. They can embrace it (and fall to Chaos Undivided, unless they
# serve the necromantic faiths) or cling to their faith while the delusion
# deepens. Only humans, dwarfs, elves, halflings and ogres are tempted.
# Faction: Generic
# Scope: ROOT is the ruler.
character_event = {
	id = lunacy.34
	desc = { text = EVTDESClunacy34 trigger = { religion = sigmarite } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESClunacy34b trigger = { NOT = { religion = sigmarite } } }
	picture = GFX_evt_lunatic
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_five_year_pulse, random list lunacy_god_delusion

	only_rulers = yes
	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no

	trigger = {
		has_character_modifier = god_delusion
		lunacy_raving_trigger = yes
		lunacy_can_act_trigger = yes
		NOT = { religion_group = chaos_gods_group }
		is_undead_trigger = no
		OR = {
			trait = creature_human
			trait = creature_dwarf
			trait = creature_elf
			trait = creature_halfling
			trait = creature_ogre
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1
		modifier = { factor = 1.33 trait = immortal }
	}

	option = { # Bow to no one
		name = EVTOPTAlunacy34
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = mutate.5 } } # Lunacy counter
		if = { limit = { NOT = { religion_group = necromantic_group } } religion = chaos }
		piety = -400
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50
			modifier = { factor = 0.25 trait = zealous }
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = cynical }
		}
	}
	option = { # Cling to the old gods
		name = EVTOPTBlunacy34
		change_variable = { which = "lunatic_counter" value = 2 }
		prestige = -100
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = mutate.5 } } # Lunacy counter
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = zealous }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = cynical }
		}
	}
}
