###############################################################################
# Vampire ageing and umbrella
###############################################################################

# lunacy.50 The weight of centuries
# An old vampire's mind frays: 7 in 10 times the lunatic counter rises (twice
# for Strigoi) and the lunacy stage is updated. Clever minds and wizards hold
# out longer; Strigoi and everchildren hardly at all. About once in 66 years,
# more often with great age.
# Faction: Vampires
# Scope: ROOT is the vampire.
character_event = {
	id = lunacy.50
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_decade_pulse, random list lunacy_vampire_aging

	min_age = 75
	capable_only = yes

	trigger = {
		is_vampire_trigger = yes
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1
		modifier = { factor = 1.1 age = 150 }
		modifier = { factor = 1.1 age = 250 }
		modifier = { factor = 1.1 age = 400 }
		modifier = { factor = 1.1 age = 600 }
		modifier = { factor = 1.25 age = 1000 }
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.4
			OR = { trait = vampire_strigoi_visible trait = vampire_strigoi_hidden }
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.25
			OR = { trait = vampire_necrarch_visible trait = vampire_necrarch_hidden }
		}
		modifier = { factor = 1.1 lunacy_everchild_body_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			30 = { # The mind holds
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 NOR = { trait = creature_human trait = creature_amazon } }
				modifier = { factor = 0.7 trait = infirm }
				modifier = { factor = 0.7 trait = addict }
				modifier = { factor = 0.8 trait = depressed }
				modifier = { factor = 0.9 trait = stressed }
				modifier = { factor = 0.9 trait = drunkard }
				modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = imbecile }
				modifier = { factor = 0.6 trait = idiot }
				modifier = { factor = 0.7 trait = slow }
				modifier = { factor = 0.8 trait = dumb }
				modifier = { factor = 0.9 trait = dim }
				modifier = { factor = 1.2 trait = bright }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = smart }
				modifier = { factor = 1.75 trait = quick }
				modifier = { factor = 2 trait = brilliant }
				modifier = { factor = 2.5 trait = genius }
				modifier = { factor = 1.25 has_character_modifier = magic_power_1 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 has_character_modifier = magic_power_2 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.75 has_character_modifier = magic_power_3 }
				modifier = { factor = 2 has_character_modifier = magic_power_4 }
				# Strigoi and everchildren have almost no defence
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.1
					OR = { trait = vampire_strigoi_visible trait = vampire_strigoi_hidden }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.9
					OR = { trait = vampire_necrarch_visible trait = vampire_necrarch_hidden }
				}
				modifier = { factor = 0.4 lunacy_everchild_body_trigger = yes }
			}
			70 = { # The mind frays
				change_variable = { which = "lunatic_counter" value = 1 }
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = { trait = vampire_strigoi_visible trait = vampire_strigoi_hidden }
					}
					change_variable = { which = "lunatic_counter" value = 1 }
				}
				lunacy_counter_check_effect = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

# lunacy.51 Vampire lunacy umbrella
# Every two years a mad vampire may suffer a twitchy quirk or, as a maniac or
# raving lunatic without a madness, catch one: Everchild Syndrome (everchildren
# only), Bloodthirsty, God Delusion, Exquisite Agonies or Heart of Melancholy.
# The bloodline, temperament and living family shift the odds; doom and
# cursed lands make madness likelier.
# Faction: Vampires
# Scope: ROOT is the vampire.
character_event = {
	id = lunacy.51
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_bi_yearly_pulse

	min_age = 76
	capable_only = yes

	trigger = {
		is_vampire_trigger = yes
		OR = { trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 }
	}

	immediate = {
		random_list = {
			10 = { # Twitchy: whispers and shadows
				modifier = { factor = 0 lunacy_twitchy_trigger = no }
				modifier = { factor = 0 trait = paranoid }
				character_event = { id = lunacy.2 }
			}
			10 = { # Twitchy: unmoved by suffering
				modifier = { factor = 0 lunacy_twitchy_trigger = no }
				modifier = { factor = 0 trait = cruel }
				character_event = { id = lunacy.3 }
			}
			5 = { # Twitchy mage: dangerous experiment
				modifier = { factor = 0 lunacy_twitchy_trigger = no }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_magic_power_trigger = no }
				character_event = { id = lunacy.5 }
			}
			10 = { # Maniac: Everchild Syndrome
				modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { trait = lunatic_2 } }
				modifier = { factor = 0 lunacy_everchild_body_trigger = no }
				modifier = { factor = 0 lunacy_has_madness_trigger = yes }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = shy }
				modifier = { factor = 1.25 is_married = no }
				modifier = { factor = 2 mother = { is_alive = yes } }
				character_event = { id = lunacy.52 }
			}
			10 = { # Maniac: Bloodthirsty
				modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { trait = lunatic_2 } }
				modifier = { factor = 0 lunacy_has_madness_trigger = yes }
				modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = cruel }
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.75
					OR = { trait = vampire_blood_visible trait = vampire_blood_hidden }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.25
					OR = { trait = vampire_strigoi_visible trait = vampire_strigoi_hidden }
				}
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = impaler }
				modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = duelist }
				character_event = { id = lunacy.14 }
			}
			10 = { # Maniac: God Delusion
				modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { trait = lunatic_2 } }
				modifier = { factor = 0 lunacy_has_madness_trigger = yes }
				modifier = { factor = 0 is_ruler = no }
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.75
					OR = { trait = vampire_jade_visible trait = vampire_jade_hidden }
				}
				modifier = { factor = 1.25 trait = proud }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = ambitious }
				modifier = { factor = 1.75 age = 500 }
				modifier = { factor = 1.75 independent = yes }
				modifier = { factor = 2 mother = { is_alive = no } father = { is_alive = no } }
				character_event = { id = lunacy.15 }
			}
			10 = { # Raving: Heart of Melancholy
				modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { trait = lunatic_3 } }
				modifier = { factor = 0 lunacy_has_madness_trigger = yes }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = stressed }
				modifier = { factor = 2.5 trait = depressed }
				modifier = { factor = 2.5 trait = widowed }
				modifier = { factor = 0.75 mother = { is_alive = yes } }
				modifier = { factor = 0.75 father = { is_alive = yes } }
				modifier = { factor = 0.95 any_spouse = { is_alive = yes } }
				modifier = { factor = 0.75 any_friend = { is_alive = yes } }
				modifier = { factor = 0.65 any_lover = { is_alive = yes } }
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.75
					OR = { trait = vampire_blood_visible trait = vampire_blood_hidden }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.25
					OR = { trait = vampire_strigoi_visible trait = vampire_strigoi_hidden }
				}
				character_event = { id = lunacy.54 }
			}
			10 = { # Raving: Exquisite Agonies
				modifier = { factor = 0 NOT = { trait = lunatic_3 } }
				modifier = { factor = 0 lunacy_has_madness_trigger = yes }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = lustful }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 trait = gluttonous }
				modifier = { factor = 1.75 trait = hedonist }
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.5
					OR = { trait = vampire_necrarch_visible trait = vampire_necrarch_hidden }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.75
					OR = { trait = vampire_blood_visible trait = vampire_blood_hidden }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.95
					OR = { trait = vampire_strigoi_visible trait = vampire_strigoi_hidden }
				}
				character_event = { id = lunacy.53 }
			}
			35 = { # Nothing this time
				# Morrslieb and doom
				modifier = { factor = 0.85 has_global_flag = morrslieb_full }
				modifier = { factor = 1.4 is_doom_very_low_trigger = yes }
				modifier = { factor = 0.95 is_doom_low_trigger = yes }
				modifier = { factor = 0.85 is_doom_medium_trigger = yes }
				modifier = { factor = 0.75 is_doom_high_trigger = yes }
				modifier = { factor = 0.5 is_doom_very_high_trigger = yes }
				# Temperament
				modifier = { factor = 0.95 trait = addict }
				modifier = { factor = 0.85 trait = lore_tzeentch }
				modifier = { factor = 0.95 trait = proud }
				modifier = { factor = 0.95 trait = ambitious }
				modifier = { factor = 0.95 trait = cruel }
				modifier = { factor = 0.75 trait = depressed }
				modifier = { factor = 0.95 trait = widowed }
				modifier = { factor = 0.85 trait = hedonist }
				# Living family and friends anchor the mind
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 mother = { is_alive = yes } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.5 father = { is_alive = yes } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.45 any_lover = { is_alive = yes } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.35 any_friend = { is_alive = yes } }
				modifier = { factor = 1.25 any_spouse = { is_alive = yes } }
				# Bloodline
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.75
					OR = { trait = vampire_strigoi_visible trait = vampire_strigoi_hidden }
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 0.85
					OR = { trait = vampire_necrarch_visible trait = vampire_necrarch_hidden }
				}
				modifier = { factor = 0.95 lunacy_everchild_body_trigger = yes }
				# The unschooled mind has no defence against the Winds of Magic
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.25
					NOR = {
						trait = magic_potential_1
						trait = magic_potential_2
						trait = magic_potential_3
						trait = magic_potential_4
					}
				}
				modifier = { factor = 1.25 has_magic_power_trigger = no }
				# Cursed lands
				modifier = { factor = 0.75 location = { region = world_chaos_wastes } }
				modifier = { factor = 0.95 location = { kingdom = { title = k_sylvania } } }
				modifier = { factor = 0.85 location = { kingdom = { title = k_mousillon } } }
				modifier = { factor = 0.95 location = { duchy = { title = d_geisterforst } } }
				modifier = { factor = 0.75 location = { has_province_modifier = warpdust_traces } }
			}
		}
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Vampire madness onsets

# lunacy.52 Everchild Syndrome
# A maniac everchild vampire stops fighting the child's body and becomes a
# child in mind as well.
# Faction: Vampires
# Scope: ROOT is the vampire.
character_event = {
	id = lunacy.52
	desc = EVTDESClunacy52
	picture = GFX_evt_feral_vampire
	is_triggered_only = yes # lunacy.51

	trigger = {
		trait = lunatic_2
		lunacy_everchild_body_trigger = yes
		lunacy_has_madness_trigger = no
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlunacy52
		if = { limit = { lunacy_sane_faith_trigger = yes } piety = -100 }
		lunacy_court_shocked_effect = yes
		change_variable = { which = "lunatic_counter" value = 2 }
		add_character_modifier = { name = everchild_syndrome duration = -1 }
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = mutate.5 } } # Lunacy counter
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Everchild Syndrome
###############################################################################

# lunacy.60 The everchild seeks its mother
# An everchild vampire with Everchild Syndrome runs to its living mother for
# comfort and blood (lunacy.61). About once in 33 years.
# Faction: Vampires
# Scope: ROOT is the everchild.
character_event = {
	id = lunacy.60
	desc = { text = EVTDESClunacy60 trigger = { mother = { lunacy_everchild_body_trigger = no } } }
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESClunacy60b
		trigger = { mother = { lunacy_everchild_body_trigger = yes } }
	}
	picture = GFX_evt_vampire_politics
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_decade_pulse, random list lunacy_everchild

	min_age = 75
	capable_only = yes
	prisoner = no

	trigger = {
		has_character_modifier = everchild_syndrome
		lunacy_can_act_trigger = yes
		mother = { is_alive = yes prisoner = no lunacy_can_act_trigger = yes }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlunacy60
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = mutate.5 } } # Lunacy counter
		mother = { character_event = { id = lunacy.61 } }
	}
}

# lunacy.61 The mother is asked
# The mother decides: let the child drink from her wrist (lunacy.62) or push it
# away (lunacy.63). A mother who is an everchild herself plays with it instead
# (lunacy.64).
# Faction: Vampires
# Scope: ROOT is the mother, FROM the everchild.
character_event = {
	id = lunacy.61
	desc = { text = EVTDESClunacy61 trigger = { lunacy_everchild_body_trigger = no } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESClunacy61b trigger = { lunacy_everchild_body_trigger = yes } }
	picture = GFX_evt_vampire_politics
	is_triggered_only = yes # lunacy.60

	option = { # Let it drink
		name = EVTOPTAlunacy61
		trigger = { lunacy_everchild_body_trigger = no }
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = lunacy.62 } } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = kind }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = cruel }
		}
	}
	option = { # Push it away
		name = EVTOPTBlunacy61
		trigger = { lunacy_everchild_body_trigger = no }
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = lunacy.63 } } }
		ai_chance = {
			factor = 50
			modifier = { factor = 2 trait = cruel }
			modifier = { factor = 0.5 trait = kind }
		}
	}
	option = { # Play with it
		name = EVTOPTClunacy61
		trigger = { lunacy_everchild_body_trigger = yes }
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = lunacy.64 } } }
	}
}

# lunacy.62 Drinking from mother
# The mother lets the everchild drink from her wrist until it sleeps. A mortal
# human mother can be turned into a vampire of the child's bloodline
# (lunacy_turn_mother_effect).
# Faction: Vampires
# Scope: ROOT is the everchild, FROM the mother.
character_event = {
	id = lunacy.62
	desc = EVTDESClunacy62
	picture = GFX_evt_vampire_politics
	is_triggered_only = yes # lunacy.61

	option = { # Turn her
		name = EVTOPTAlunacy62
		trigger = { FROM = { trait = creature_human is_vampire_trigger = no } }
		lunacy_turn_mother_effect = yes
		ai_chance = { factor = 30 }
	}
	option = { # Just drink
		name = EVTOPTBlunacy62
		ai_chance = { factor = 70 }
	}
}

# lunacy.63 Pushed away
# The mother refused; the everchild sulks.
# Faction: Vampires
# Scope: ROOT is the everchild, FROM the mother.
character_event = {
	id = lunacy.63
	desc = EVTDESClunacy63
	picture = GFX_evt_vampire_politics
	is_triggered_only = yes # lunacy.61

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlunacy63
		opinion = { modifier = opinion_annoyed who = FROM }
	}
}

# lunacy.64 Two children at play
# A mother who is an everchild herself plays with her child all night. They
# become friends.
# Faction: Vampires
# Scope: ROOT is the mother, FROM the everchild.
character_event = {
	id = lunacy.64
	desc = EVTDESClunacy64
	picture = GFX_evt_vampire_politics
	is_triggered_only = yes # lunacy.61

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlunacy64
		add_friend = FROM
	}
}

# lunacy.65 Partner of an everchild
# The lover or spouse of a vampire with Everchild Syndrome no longer knows how
# to be with them. A lover can end the affair; a spouse can stay for politics.
# The everchild is told (lunacy.66 or lunacy.67). Once per character.
# Faction: Vampires
# Scope: ROOT is the partner; event target lunacy_everchild is the everchild.
character_event = {
	id = lunacy.65
	desc = { text = EVTDESClunacy65 trigger = { is_vampire_trigger = no } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESClunacy65b trigger = { is_vampire_trigger = yes } }
	picture = GFX_evt_vampire_politics
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_five_year_pulse, random list lunacy_everchild_partner

	min_age = 16
	capable_only = yes

	trigger = {
		lunacy_everchild_partner_trigger = yes
		lunacy_can_act_trigger = yes
		lunacy_everchild_body_trigger = no
		NOT = { has_character_flag = lunacy_everchild_partner_done }
	}

	immediate = {
		set_character_flag = lunacy_everchild_partner_done
		if = {
			limit = { any_lover = { has_character_modifier = everchild_syndrome } }
			random_lover = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = everchild_syndrome }
				save_event_target_as = lunacy_everchild
			}
		}
		else = {
			random_spouse = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = everchild_syndrome }
				save_event_target_as = lunacy_everchild
			}
		}
	}

	option = { # I cannot love a child
		name = EVTOPTAlunacy65
		event_target:lunacy_everchild = {
			character_event = { id = lunacy.66 }
			if = { limit = { is_lover = ROOT } remove_lover = ROOT }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
	}
	option = { # It was never about love
		name = EVTOPTBlunacy65
		trigger = { any_spouse = { character = event_target:lunacy_everchild } }
		event_target:lunacy_everchild = { character_event = { id = lunacy.67 } }
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 modifier = { factor = 2 trait = ambitious } }
	}
}

# lunacy.66 No longer loved

# Faction: Vampires
# Scope: ROOT is the everchild, FROM the partner.
character_event = {
	id = lunacy.66
	desc = { text = EVTDESClunacy66 trigger = { FROM = { is_vampire_trigger = no } } }
	desc = { text = EVTDESClunacy66b trigger = { FROM = { is_vampire_trigger = yes } } }
	picture = GFX_evt_vampire_politics
	is_triggered_only = yes # lunacy.65

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlunacy66
		opinion = { modifier = opinion_annoyed who = FROM }
	}
}

# lunacy.70 A taste for excess

# a hedonist.

	id = lunacy.70

	}

# lunacy.71 Something new
# A hedonist vampire with Exquisite Agonies picks a new way to feel something:
# pain (lunacy.72), feeding (lunacy.73-74), lust (lunacy.75-78) or the
# vampire's own banes (lunacy.79-82).
# Faction: Vampires
# Scope: ROOT is the vampire.
character_event = {
	id = lunacy.71
	desc = EVTDESClunacy71
	picture = GFX_evt_vampire_politics
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_five_year_pulse, random list lunacy_exquisite

	min_age = 75
	capable_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_character_modifier = exquisite_agonies
		lunacy_can_act_trigger = yes
		trait = hedonist
	}

	option = { # Pain
		name = EVTOPTAlunacy71
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = lunacy.72 days = 2 random = 2 } }
	}
	option = { # Feeding
		name = EVTOPTBlunacy71
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				50 = { character_event = { id = lunacy.73 days = 2 random = 2 } }
				50 = { character_event = { id = lunacy.74 days = 2 random = 2 } }
			}
		}
	}
	option = { # Lust
		name = EVTOPTClunacy71
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				25 = { character_event = { id = lunacy.75 days = 2 random = 2 } }
				25 = { character_event = { id = lunacy.76 days = 2 random = 2 } }
				25 = { character_event = { id = lunacy.77 days = 2 random = 2 } }
				25 = { character_event = { id = lunacy.78 days = 2 random = 2 } }
			}
		}
	}
	option = { # Anything
		name = EVTOPTDlunacy71
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				25 = { character_event = { id = lunacy.79 days = 2 random = 2 } }
				25 = { character_event = { id = lunacy.80 days = 2 random = 2 } }
				25 = { character_event = { id = lunacy.81 days = 2 random = 2 } }
				25 = { character_event = { id = lunacy.82 days = 2 random = 2 } }
			}
		}
	}
}

# lunacy.72 Cutting
# The vampire cuts their own flesh: wounded, maybe maimed, and a maimed one
# may end up disfigured.
# Faction: Vampires
# Scope: ROOT is the vampire.
character_event = {
	id = lunacy.72
	desc = EVTDESClunacy72
	picture = GFX_evt_feral_vampire
	is_triggered_only = yes # lunacy.71

	trigger = {
		has_character_modifier = exquisite_agonies
		lunacy_can_act_trigger = yes
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlunacy72
		if = {
			limit = { trait = maimed }
			random = { chance = 40 remove_trait = maimed add_trait = disfigured }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { OR = { trait = scarred trait = wounded } }
			random = { chance = 10 add_trait = maimed }
		}
		else = { add_trait = wounded }
	}
}

# lunacy.74 Eating flesh
# The vampire eats the flesh of the living like a ghoul. Proud bloodlines at
# court are disgusted, and the madness deepens (less so for Strigoi, who are
# used to it).
# Faction: Vampires
# Scope: ROOT is the vampire.
character_event = {
	id = lunacy.74
	desc = EVTDESClunacy74
	picture = GFX_evt_feral_vampire
	is_triggered_only = yes # lunacy.71

	trigger = {
		has_character_modifier = exquisite_agonies
		lunacy_can_act_trigger = yes
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlunacy74
		piety = -50
		any_vassal = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					trait = vampire_lahmian_visible
					trait = vampire_carstein_visible
					trait = vampire_blood_visible
					trait = vampire_necrarch_visible
					trait = vampire_mahtmasi_visible
				}
			}
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_depraved_undead who = ROOT }
		}
		any_courtier = {
			limit = {
				is_ruler = no
				OR = {
					trait = vampire_lahmian_visible
					trait = vampire_carstein_visible
					trait = vampire_blood_visible
					trait = vampire_necrarch_visible
					trait = vampire_mahtmasi_visible
				}
			}
			opinion = { modifier = opinion_depraved_undead who = ROOT }
		}
		hidden_tooltip = {
			character_event = { id = mutate.5 } # Lunacy counter
			random_list = {
				70 = { change_variable = { which = "lunatic_counter" value = 2 } }
				30 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 2.5
						OR = { trait = vampire_strigoi_visible trait = vampire_strigoi_hidden }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		lunacy_gluttony_effect = yes
	}
}

# lunacy.87 Into the long sleep
# A vampire with Heart of Melancholy withdraws into hibernation: gains the
# resting trait, and a ruler's capital takes hidden_hibernated_strigoi
# (vamp.60 clears it once they wake). Every few years they may wake
# (lunacy.88). About once in 30 years.
# Faction: Vampires
# Scope: ROOT is the vampire.
character_event = {
	id = lunacy.87
	desc = EVTDESClunacy87
	picture = GFX_evt_feral_vampire
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_decade_pulse, random list lunacy_hibernation

	min_age = 75
	capable_only = yes

	trigger = {
		has_character_modifier = heart_of_melancholy
		lunacy_can_act_trigger = yes
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAlunacy87
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = mutate.5 } } # Lunacy counter
		add_trait = resting
		if = {
			limit = { is_ruler = yes }
			capital_scope = {
				add_province_modifier = { name = hidden_hibernated_strigoi duration = -1 }
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = lunacy.88 days = 100 random = 900 } } # Hibernation clock
	}
}

# lunacy.88 Stirring in the crypt
# The sleeping vampire may wake (losing resting and the capital modifier) or
# sleep on, gaining a little piety, and be asked again later.
# Faction: Vampires
# Scope: ROOT is the vampire.
character_event = {
	id = lunacy.88
	desc = EVTDESClunacy88
	picture = GFX_evt_feral_vampire
	is_triggered_only = yes # lunacy.87, lunacy.88

	trigger = {
		trait = resting
	}

	option = { # Wake
		name = EVTOPTAlunacy88
		remove_trait = resting
		if = {
			limit = { is_ruler = yes }
			capital_scope = { remove_province_modifier = hidden_hibernated_strigoi }
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 70 }
	}
	option = { # Sleep on
		name = EVTOPTBlunacy88
		piety = 150
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = lunacy.88 days = 100 random = 900 } } # Hibernation clock
		ai_chance = { factor = 30 }
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Recovery
###############################################################################

# lunacy.90 The mind mends
# A sane character slowly sheds the strain that builds toward lunacy: the
# lunatic counter drops by 1. Without this the counter only ever grew, so a
# dwarf or elf living for centuries was all but sure to go mad. Long-lived and
# hardy races (lizardmen, greenskins) mend faster.
# Scope: ROOT is the character.
character_event = {
	id = lunacy.90
	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes # on_five_year_pulse, random list lunacy_recovery

	trigger = {
		is_lunatic_trigger = no
		check_variable = { which = "lunatic_counter" value = 1 }
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		factor = 1
		modifier = { factor = 2 lifespan_dwarf_trigger = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 2 lifespan_elf_trigger = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 2 lifespan_other_long_lived_trigger = yes }
		modifier = { factor = 3 lifespan_hardy_race_trigger = yes }
	}

	immediate = {
		change_variable = { which = "lunatic_counter" value = -1 }
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Maimed events (namespace maimed)
# Faction: Generic
# Serves: anyone who loses an eye, a hand or a leg, is disfigured or
# mangled, and the victims of mad cannibals.
#
#   maimed.1-5      The maiming notices: one-eyed, one-handed, one-legged,
#                   disfigured, mangled
#   maimed.6-7      Missing an eye, a hand and a leg, a ruler gets a parrot
#   maimed.10-11    A mad cannibal eats a prisoner
#   maimed.100-114  Disfigured: a golden mask, the children, a masquerade
#                   (a mad host may make masks the law)
#   maimed.200-205  One-eyed: a thrown rock, a glass eye
#   maimed.300-305  One-handed: training the other hand, a false hand
#   maimed.400-404  One-legged: a dance, a wooden leg
#   maimed.500-504  Phantom pain and the physician's cures
#   maimed.505-511  A close relation nurses the injured
#
# maimed.100-511 fire from the on_yearly_pulse random list; they were the
# maimed part of rip_flavor_events.txt (RIP.29100-29511 = maimed.N-29000).
#
# Fired by the maiming effects in 00_scripted_effects.txt, torture, the
# dancing plague (dancing.9) and others.
# Was events/rip_maimed_events.txt (RIP.11501-11511): maimed.N is RIP.N-11500.
###############################################################################

namespace = maimed

# maimed.1 One-eyed
# Tells the character they lost an eye.
# Scope: ROOT is the maimed character. Fired by the maiming effects, torture
# and flavour events.
character_event = {
	id = maimed.1
	desc = EVTDESCmaimed1
	picture = GFX_evt_doctor_tending_to_sick

	only_playable = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	ai = no
	hide_from = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAmaimed1
		tooltip = { add_trait = one_eyed }
	}
}

# maimed.6 Starting fresh
# A ruler missing an eye, a hand and a leg finds animals no longer fear them,
# and a parrot comes to stay (maimed.7).
# Scope: ROOT is the player character. Mean time to happen, players only.
narrative_event = {
	id = maimed.6
	title = EVTNAMEmaimed6
	desc = EVTDESCmaimed6
	picture = GFX_evt_lunatic

	only_playable = yes
	ai = no

	trigger = {
		trait = one_eyed
		trait = one_handed
		trait = one_legged
		NOT = { has_character_modifier = pet_parrot }
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 24
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAmaimed6
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = maimed.7 } }
	}
}

# maimed.7 A parrot
# Adds the pet_parrot modifier.
# Scope: ROOT is the character. Fired by maimed.6.

	id = maimed.7
	desc = EVTDESCmaimed7
	picture = GFX_evt_lunatic

	only_playable = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes
	ai = no

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAmaimed7
		add_character_modifier = { name = pet_parrot duration = -1 }
	}
}

# maimed.10 The cannibal feasts
# A mad cannibal eats a prisoner and takes in some of their traits and
# strength.
# Scope: ROOT is the cannibal, FROM the victim. Fired by the torture decision.
character_event = {
	id = maimed.10
	picture = GFX_evt_impalement
	desc = EVTDESCmaimed10
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAmaimed10

		### Congenital traits
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { trait = genius } NOT = { trait = genius } }
			if = { limit = { trait = imbecile } remove_trait = imbecile }
			if = { limit = { trait = slow } remove_trait = slow }
			if = { limit = { trait = dull } remove_trait = dull }
			if = { limit = { trait = quick } remove_trait = quick }
			add_trait = genius
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { trait = quick } NOT = { trait = genius } NOT = { trait = quick } }
			if = { limit = { trait = imbecile } remove_trait = imbecile }
			if = { limit = { trait = slow } remove_trait = slow }
			if = { limit = { trait = dull } remove_trait = dull }
			add_trait = quick
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { trait = strong } NOT = { trait = strong } }
			if = { limit = { trait = weak } remove_trait = weak }
			if = { limit = { trait = feeble } remove_trait = feeble }
			add_trait = strong
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { trait = fair } NOT = { trait = fair } }
			if = { limit = { trait = ugly } remove_trait = ugly }
			add_trait = fair
		}

		### Personality traits - Sins and virtues
		if = { limit = { FROM = { trait = chaste } NOT = { trait = chaste } } add_trait = chaste }
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { trait = lustful } NOT = { trait = lustful } }
			add_trait = lustful
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { trait = temperate } NOT = { trait = temperate } }
			add_trait = temperate
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { trait = gluttonous } NOT = { trait = gluttonous } }
			add_trait = gluttonous
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { trait = charitable } NOT = { trait = charitable } }
			add_trait = charitable
		}
		if = { limit = { FROM = { trait = greedy } NOT = { trait = greedy } } add_trait = greedy }
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { trait = diligent } NOT = { trait = diligent } }
			add_trait = diligent
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { trait = slothful } NOT = { trait = slothful } }
			add_trait = slothful
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { trait = patient } NOT = { trait = patient } }
			add_trait = patient
		}
		if = { limit = { FROM = { trait = wroth } NOT = { trait = wroth } } add_trait = wroth }
		if = { limit = { FROM = { trait = kind } NOT = { trait = kind } } add_trait = kind }
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { trait = envious } NOT = { trait = envious } }
			add_trait = envious
		}
		if = { limit = { FROM = { trait = humble } NOT = { trait = humble } } add_trait = humble }
		if = { limit = { FROM = { trait = proud } NOT = { trait = proud } } add_trait = proud }

		### Personality traits - Other
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { trait = deceitful } NOT = { trait = deceitful } }
			add_trait = deceitful
		}
		if = { limit = { FROM = { trait = honest } NOT = { trait = honest } } add_trait = honest }
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { trait = craven } NOR = { trait = craven trait = berserker } }
			add_trait = craven
		}
		if = { limit = { FROM = { trait = brave } NOT = { trait = brave } } add_trait = brave }
		if = { limit = { FROM = { trait = shy } NOT = { trait = shy } } add_trait = shy }
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { trait = gregarious } NOT = { trait = gregarious } }
			add_trait = gregarious
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { trait = ambitious } NOT = { trait = ambitious } }
			add_trait = ambitious
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { trait = content } NOT = { trait = content } }
			add_trait = content
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { trait = arbitrary } NOT = { trait = arbitrary } }
			add_trait = arbitrary
		}
		if = { limit = { FROM = { trait = just } NOT = { trait = just } } add_trait = just }
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { trait = cynical } NOT = { trait = cynical } }
			add_trait = cynical
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { trait = zealous } NOT = { trait = zealous } }
			add_trait = zealous
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { trait = paranoid } NOT = { trait = paranoid } }
			add_trait = paranoid
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { trait = trusting } NOT = { trait = trusting } }
			add_trait = trusting
		}
		if = { limit = { FROM = { trait = cruel } NOT = { trait = cruel } } add_trait = cruel }
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { trait = erudite } NOT = { trait = erudite } }
			add_trait = erudite
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { trait = stubborn } NOT = { trait = stubborn } }
			add_trait = stubborn
		}

		### Other traits
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { immortal = yes } immortal = no }
			add_trait = immortal
			if = { limit = { trait = incapable } remove_trait = incapable }
			if = {
				limit = { is_female = no has_dlc = "Holy Fury" }
				create_bloodline = { type = immortal_bloodline_01 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { is_female = yes has_dlc = "Holy Fury" }
				create_bloodline = { type = immortal_bloodline_01 inheritance = matrilineal }
			}
		}
	}
}

# maimed.11 Eaten by a cannibal
# A close relative of the victim learns they were eaten.
# Scope: ROOT is the relative, FROM the victim, FROMFROM the cannibal. Fired
# by the torture decision and Tamurkhan.
character_event = {
	id = maimed.11
	picture = GFX_evt_impalement
	desc = EVTDESCmaimed11
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAmaimed11
		add_rival = FROMFROM
		opinion = { who = FROMFROM modifier = opinion_killed_close_kin months = 60 }
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Disfigured (maimed.100-114)
###############################################################################

# maimed.100 Disfigured
# Hidden. Picks a disfigured event: the golden mask, the children, or a
# masquerade.
# Scope: ROOT is a disfigured character. Fired by the on_yearly_pulse random
# list.
character_event = {
	id = maimed.100

	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes

	only_playable = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = disfigured
		NOT = { trait = severely_injured }
		block_general_event_trigger = no
	}

	option = {
		random_list = {
			#Golden mask
			10 = {
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					OR = { any_lover = { is_alive = yes } has_character_modifier = golden_mask }
				}
				character_event = { id = maimed.101 }
			}
			#Scaring the children
			10 = {
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					NOR = {
						is_married = yes
						any_child = {
							age = 2
							NOT = { age = 13 }
							NOT = { trait = brave }
							NOT = { trait = willful }
							NOT = { trait = rowdy }
						}
					}
				}
				random_spouse = {
					limit = {
						any_child = {
							age = 2
							NOT = { age = 13 }
							NOT = { trait = brave }
							NOT = { trait = willful }
							NOT = { trait = rowdy }
						}
					}
					character_event = { id = maimed.102 }
				}
			}
			#Phantom pain
			10 = {
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					OR = {
						NOT = {
							any_courtier_or_vassal = { has_minor_title = title_court_physician }
						}
						has_character_modifier = phantom_pain
					}
				}
				character_event = { id = maimed.500 }
			}
			#Masquerade
			10 = {
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					OR = {
						any_courtier = { has_character_modifier = forced_mask }
						any_courtier = { has_character_modifier = attending_masquerade }
					}
				}
				character_event = { id = maimed.104 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# maimed.101 A golden mask
# Suitors shy away; a fine mask could help.
# Scope: ROOT is the disfigured character. Fired by maimed.100.
character_event = {
	id = maimed.101
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy
	picture = GFX_evt_courtiers_talking

	desc = { trigger = { prefers_men_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCmaimed101_A }
	desc = { trigger = { prefers_men_trigger = no } text = EVTDESCmaimed101_B }

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = { trigger = { prefers_men_trigger = yes } text = EVTOPTmaimed101_A_A }
		name = { trigger = { prefers_men_trigger = no } text = EVTOPTmaimed101_A_B }

		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTmaimed101_A_TOOLTIP }

		scaled_wealth = { value = -0.2 min = -25 }
		add_character_modifier = { name = golden_mask duration = -1 }
		hidden_tooltip = { remove_trait = disfigured add_trait = disfigured }
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTmaimed101_B
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTmaimed101_B_TOOLTIP }
	}
}

# maimed.102 Scaring the children
# Hidden. The spouse brings up the children's fear (103).
# Scope: ROOT is the spouse, FROM the disfigured character. Fired by
# maimed.100.
character_event = {
	id = maimed.102

	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes

	immediate = {
		random_child = {
			limit = {
				age = 2
				NOT = { age = 13 }
				NOT = { trait = brave }
				NOT = { trait = willful }
				NOT = { trait = rowdy }
			}
			save_event_target_as = rip_scared_child
		}
	}

	option = {
		FROM = { character_event = { id = maimed.103 } }
	}
}

# maimed.103 The children are scared
# The spouse says the face scares the children.
# Scope: ROOT is the disfigured character, FROM the spouse. Fired by
# maimed.102.
character_event = {
	id = maimed.103
	desc = EVTDESCmaimed103
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy
	picture = GFX_evt_child_play

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTmaimed103_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTmaimed103_A_TOOLTIP }

		reverse_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_did_not_take_my_advice }

		random = {
			chance = 50

			event_target:rip_scared_child = {
				opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_repulsed_deformity }

				if = {
					limit = {
						NOR = { trait = craven trait = berserker }
						NOT = { has_dlc = "Zeus" }
					}
					add_trait = craven
				}
				if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = timid } has_dlc = "Zeus" } add_trait = timid }
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTmaimed103_B
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTmaimed103_B_TOOLTIP }

		random = {
			chance = 50
			add_trait = depressed
			hidden_tooltip = {
				character_event = {
					id = 38288 #Notify Depressed
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# maimed.104 A masquerade
# A masked ruler can hold a masquerade ball where everyone wears masks.
# Scope: ROOT is the disfigured ruler. Fired by maimed.100.
character_event = {
	id = maimed.104
	desc = EVTDESCmaimed104
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy
	picture = GFX_evt_scandal

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTmaimed104_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTmaimed104_A_TOOLTIP }
		scaled_wealth = { value = -0.25 min = -25 }

		hidden_tooltip = {
			set_character_flag = do_not_disturb

			#Invitations
			any_courtier = { character_event = { id = maimed.112 days = 3 } }
			any_vassal = { character_event = { id = maimed.112 days = 3 } }

			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = { trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 trait = envious }
				}
				character_event = { id = maimed.105 days = 20 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOR = { trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 trait = envious }
				}
				character_event = { id = maimed.110 days = 20 }
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTmaimed104_B
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTmaimed104_B_TOOLTIP }
	}
}

# maimed.112 Invited to the masquerade
# A vassal is invited to the liege's masquerade.
# Scope: ROOT is the guest, FROM the host. Fired by maimed.104.
character_event = {
	id = maimed.112
	desc = EVTDESCmaimed112
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy
	picture = GFX_evt_scandal

	desc = {
		trigger = { has_dlc = "Way of Life" }
		picture = GFX_evt_dancing
		text = EVTDESCmaimed112
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { NOT = { has_dlc = "Way of Life" } }
		picture = GFX_evt_courtiers_talking
		text = EVTDESCmaimed112
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	#Attend
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTmaimed112_A

		add_character_modifier = { name = attending_masquerade duration = -1 }

		set_character_flag = do_not_disturb

		character_event = { id = maimed.114 days = 100 }

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 30
			modifier = {
				factor = 2
				OR = {
					trait = gregarious
					trait = disfigured
					trait = hedonist
					trait = gluttonous
					trait = socializer
				}
			}
		}
	}

	#Decline
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTmaimed112_B

		reverse_opinion = { modifier = opinion_declined_party who = FROM years = 10 }

		hidden_tooltip = { remove_character_modifier = attending_masquerade }
		ai_chance = { factor = 1 modifier = { factor = 20 OR = { trait = shy trait = paranoid } } }
	}
}

# maimed.105 A mad masquerade
# A lunatic host may decree that the masks must stay on for ever.
# Scope: ROOT is the host. Fired by maimed.104.
character_event = {
	id = maimed.105
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	picture = GFX_evt_suspicious_noble
	desc = {
		trigger = { has_dlc = "Way of Life" }
		picture = GFX_evt_dancing
		text = EVTDESCmaimed105
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { NOT = { has_dlc = "Way of Life" } }
		picture = GFX_evt_suspicious_noble
		text = EVTDESCmaimed105
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	#YES (lunatic)
	option = {
		trigger = { OR = { trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 } }
		name = EVTOPTmaimed105_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTmaimed105_A_TOOLTIP }

		tooltip_info_custom = lunatic

		if = {
			limit = { NOR = { trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 } }
			random = {
				chance = 50
				add_trait = lunatic_1
				set_variable = { which = "lunatic_counter" value = 6 }
				character_event = { id = 38304 }
			}
		}

		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = maimed.106 } }
	}

	#YES (envious)
	option = {
		trigger = {
			trait = envious
			NOR = { trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 }
		}
		name = EVTOPTmaimed105_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTmaimed105_A_TOOLTIP }

		tooltip_info = envious

		if = {
			limit = { NOR = { trait = lunatic_1 trait = lunatic_2 trait = lunatic_3 } }
			random = {
				chance = 50
				add_trait = lunatic_1
				set_variable = { which = "lunatic_counter" value = 6 }
				character_event = { id = 38304 }
			}
		}

		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = maimed.106 } }
	}

	#Nah
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTmaimed105_B
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTmaimed105_B_TOOLTIP }

		hidden_tooltip = {
			any_realm_character = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = attending_masquerade }
				opinion = { modifier = opinion_great_masquerade who = ROOT years = 10 }

				character_event = { id = maimed.113 }

				remove_character_modifier = attending_masquerade
			}
		}
	}
}

# maimed.106 Masks for ever
# The mad host announces masks are now law.
# Scope: ROOT is the host. Fired by maimed.105.
character_event = {
	id = maimed.106
	desc = EVTDESCmaimed106
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	picture = GFX_evt_scandal

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTmaimed106_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTmaimed106_A_TOOLTIP }

		hidden_tooltip = {
			clr_character_flag = do_not_disturb
			set_character_flag = crazy_masked_ruler
			save_event_target_as = rip_masked_ruler

			any_realm_character = {
				limit = { has_character_modifier = attending_masquerade }

				character_event = { id = maimed.107 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# maimed.107 The guests hear the decree
# The guests react to the mad law of masks.
# Scope: ROOT is the guest, FROM the host. Fired by maimed.106.
character_event = {
	id = maimed.107
	desc = EVTDESCmaimed107
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_intrigue
	picture = GFX_evt_scandal

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTmaimed107_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTmaimed107_A_TOOLTIP }

		hidden_tooltip = {
			clr_character_flag = do_not_disturb
			remove_character_modifier = attending_masquerade
			character_event = { id = maimed.108 days = 100 }
		}

		opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_insane_ruler months = 1200 }

		add_character_modifier = { name = forced_mask duration = -1 }
	}
}

# maimed.108 Is the mad host alive?
# Hidden. Checks whether the mask law still stands.
# Scope: ROOT is the guest. Fired by maimed.107.
character_event = {
	id = maimed.108

	hide_window = yes
	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		if = {
			limit = { liege = { has_character_flag = crazy_masked_ruler } }
			character_event = { id = maimed.108 days = 100 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { liege = { NOT = { has_character_flag = crazy_masked_ruler } } }
			character_event = { id = maimed.109 }
		}
	}
}

# maimed.109 The masks come off
# The mad host has died; the guests may take off their masks.
# Scope: ROOT is the guest. Fired by maimed.108.
character_event = {
	id = maimed.109
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy
	picture = GFX_evt_feast

	desc = {
		trigger = { event_target:rip_masked_ruler = { is_alive = no } }
		text = EVTDESCmaimed109_A
	}

	desc = {
		trigger = { event_target:rip_masked_ruler = { is_alive = yes } }
		text = EVTDESCmaimed109_B
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		remove_character_modifier = forced_mask
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTmaimed109_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTmaimed109_A_TOOLTIP }

		tooltip = { remove_character_modifier = forced_mask }
	}
}

		}

}

# maimed.114 Masquerade cleanup
# Hidden. Clears attending_masquerade.
# Scope: ROOT is the guest. Fired by maimed.112.
character_event = {
	id = maimed.114

	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes

	option = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_modifier = attending_masquerade }
			remove_character_modifier = attending_masquerade
		}
		clr_character_flag = do_not_disturb
	}
}

###############################################################################
# One-eyed (maimed.200-205)
###############################################################################

# maimed.200 One-eyed
# Hidden. Picks a one-eyed event.
# Scope: ROOT is a one-eyed character. Fired by the on_yearly_pulse random
# list.
character_event = {
	id = maimed.200

	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes

	only_playable = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = one_eyed
		NOT = { trait = severely_injured }
		block_general_event_trigger = no
	}

	option = {
		random_list = {
			#Rock thrown at you
			10 = {
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					NOR = {
						any_child = { age = 4 NOT = { age = 16 } at_location = ROOT }
						any_courtier = { age = 4 NOT = { age = 16 } }
					}
				}
				character_event = { id = maimed.201 }
			}
			#Glass eye
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_character_modifier = glass_eye }
				character_event = { id = maimed.205 }
			}
			#Phantom pain
			10 = {
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					OR = {
						NOT = {
							any_courtier_or_vassal = { has_minor_title = title_court_physician }
						}
						has_character_modifier = phantom_pain
					}
				}
				character_event = { id = maimed.500 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# maimed.201 A thrown rock
# A child's rock flies at the one-eyed character: catch it or duck.
# Scope: ROOT is the one-eyed character. Fired by maimed.200.
character_event = {
	id = maimed.201
	desc = EVTDESCmaimed201
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	picture = GFX_evt_child_bully

	is_triggered_only = yes

	immediate = {
		random_child = {
			limit = { age = 4 NOT = { age = 16 } at_location = ROOT }
			save_event_target_as = rip_throwing_child
		}

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { event_target:rip_throwing_child = { always = yes } } }
			random_courtier = {
				limit = { age = 4 NOT = { age = 16 } }
				save_event_target_as = rip_throwing_child
			}
		}
	}

	#Catch
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTmaimed201_A

		random_list = {
			#Success
			60 = {
				custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTmaimed201_A_SUCCESS }
				hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = maimed.202 } }
			}

			#Fail
			40 = {
				custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTmaimed201_A_FAILURE }
				hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = maimed.203 } }
			}
		}
	}

	#Throw yourself at ground
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTmaimed201_B
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTmaimed201_B_TOOLTIP }

		if = {
			limit = { NOR = { trait = craven trait = berserker } }

			random = { chance = 20 add_trait = craven set_character_flag = became_craven }
		}
		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = maimed.204 } }
	}
}

# maimed.202 Caught
# The rock is caught, to applause.
# Scope: ROOT is the one-eyed character. Fired by maimed.201.
character_event = {
	id = maimed.202
	desc = EVTDESCmaimed202
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	picture = GFX_evt_guardian

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTmaimed202_A

		prestige = 25
		change_martial = 1

		reverse_opinion = {
			who = event_target:rip_throwing_child
			modifier = opinion_impressed
			years = 5
		}
	}
}

# maimed.203 Missed
# The catch is misjudged.
# Scope: ROOT is the one-eyed character. Fired by maimed.201.
character_event = {
	id = maimed.203
	desc = EVTDESCmaimed203
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	picture = GFX_evt_doctor_tending_to_sick

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTmaimed203_A

		hidden_tooltip = { remove_trait = one_eyed }
		add_trait = blinded
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { trait = severely_injured } }
			add_trait = severely_injured
			resolve_severely_injured_effect = yes
		}

		reverse_opinion = {
			who = event_target:rip_throwing_child
			modifier = opinion_afraid
			years = 10
		}
	}
}

# maimed.204 Ducked
# Diving to the ground looks foolish.
# Scope: ROOT is the one-eyed character. Fired by maimed.201.
character_event = {
	id = maimed.204
	desc = EVTDESCmaimed204
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_war
	picture = GFX_evt_scandal

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTmaimed204_A

		prestige = -25

		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = became_craven }
			tooltip = { add_trait = craven }
			clr_character_flag = became_craven
		}

		reverse_opinion = { who = event_target:rip_throwing_child modifier = opinion_scolded }
	}
}

# maimed.205 A glass eye
# A false eye might restore confidence.
# Scope: ROOT is the one-eyed character. Fired by maimed.200.
character_event = {
	id = maimed.205
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy
	picture = GFX_evt_courtiers_talking

	desc = { trigger = { prefers_men_trigger = yes } text = EVTDESCmaimed205_A }
	desc = { trigger = { prefers_men_trigger = no } text = EVTDESCmaimed205_B }

	is_triggered_only = yes

	#Get eye made
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTmaimed205_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTmaimed205_A_TOOLTIP }

		scaled_wealth = { value = -0.1 min = -15 }

		add_character_modifier = { name = glass_eye duration = -1 }
		hidden_tooltip = { remove_trait = one_eyed add_trait = one_eyed }
	}
	#Don't get eye made
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTmaimed101_B
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTmaimed101_B_TOOLTIP }
	}
}

# One-handed (maimed.300-305)

# maimed.300 One-handed
# Hidden. Picks a one-handed event.
# Scope: ROOT is a one-handed character. Fired by the on_yearly_pulse random
# list.
character_event = {
	id = maimed.300

	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes

	only_playable = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = one_handed
		NOT = { trait = severely_injured }
		block_general_event_trigger = no
	}

	option = {
		random_list = {
			#Becoming ambidextrous
			10 = {
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					OR = {
						has_character_modifier = martially_ambidextrous
						has_character_modifier = iron_hand
						trait = blinded
					}
				}
				character_event = { id = maimed.301 }
			}
			#Getting a prosthetic hand
			10 = {
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					OR = { has_character_modifier = iron_hand has_character_modifier = golden_hand }
				}
				character_event = { id = maimed.305 }
			}
			#Phantom pain
			10 = {
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					OR = {
						NOT = {
							any_courtier_or_vassal = { has_minor_title = title_court_physician }
						}
						has_character_modifier = phantom_pain
					}
				}
				character_event = { id = maimed.500 }
			}
		}
	}
}

			}

					factor = 0
					NAND = {
						trait = one_legged
						trait = one_eyed
						trait = disfigured
						trait = mangled
					}
				}
				add_trait = wounded

			}

###############################################################################
# One-legged (maimed.400-404)
###############################################################################

# maimed.400 One-legged
# Hidden. Picks a one-legged event.
# Scope: ROOT is a one-legged character. Fired by the on_yearly_pulse random
# list.
character_event = {
	id = maimed.400

	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_window = yes

	only_playable = yes

	trigger = {
		trait = one_legged
		NOT = { trait = severely_injured }
		block_general_event_trigger = no
	}

	option = {
		random_list = {
			#Dancing
			10 = {
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					prefers_men_trigger = yes
					NOT = {
						any_courtier = {
							is_female = no
							block_general_event_trigger = no
							NOT = { is_lover = ROOT }
							NOT = { is_close_relative = ROOT }
						}
					}
				}
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					prefers_men_trigger = no
					NOT = {
						any_courtier = {
							is_female = yes
							block_general_event_trigger = no
							NOT = { is_lover = ROOT }
							NOT = { is_close_relative = ROOT }
						}
					}
				}
				character_event = { id = maimed.401 }
			}
			#Prosthesis
			10 = {
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					OR = {
						has_character_modifier = prosthetic_leg
						has_character_modifier = peg_leg
					}
				}
				character_event = { id = maimed.404 }
			}
			#Phantom pain
			10 = {
				modifier = {
					factor = 0
					OR = {
						NOT = {
							any_courtier_or_vassal = { has_minor_title = title_court_physician }
						}
						has_character_modifier = phantom_pain
					}
				}
				character_event = { id = maimed.500 }
			}
		}
	}
}

# maimed.401 A dance
# The love interest wants to dance: risk it or not.
# Scope: ROOT is the one-legged character. Fired by maimed.400.
character_event = {
	id = maimed.401
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_diplomacy
	picture = GFX_evt_courtiers_talking

	desc = {
		trigger = { has_dlc = "Way of Life" }
		picture = GFX_evt_dancing
		text = EVTDESCmaimed401
	}
	desc = {
		trigger = { NOT = { has_dlc = "Way of Life" } }
		picture = GFX_evt_courtiers_talking
		text = EVTDESCmaimed401
	}

	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = { prefers_men_trigger = yes }

			random_courtier = {
				limit = {
					is_female = no
					block_general_event_trigger = no
					NOT = { is_lover = ROOT }
					NOT = { is_close_relative = ROOT }
				}
				save_event_target_as = rip_love_interest
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { prefers_men_trigger = no }

			random_courtier = {
				limit = {
					is_female = yes
					block_general_event_trigger = no
					NOT = { is_lover = ROOT }
					NOT = { is_close_relative = ROOT }
				}
				save_event_target_as = rip_love_interest
			}
		}
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	#Try to dance
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTmaimed401_A

		random_list = {
			60 = { character_event = { id = maimed.402 tooltip = EVTOPTmaimed401_A_SUCCESS } }
			40 = { character_event = { id = maimed.403 tooltip = EVTOPTmaimed401_A_FAILURE } }
		}
	}

	#Avoid
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTmaimed401_B
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTmaimed401_B_TOOLTIP }

		random = {
			chance = 33
			add_trait = depressed
			hidden_tooltip = {
				character_event = {
					id = 38288 #Notify Depressed
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

			limit = { has_minor_title = title_court_jester }
			save_event_target_as = jester

			limit = { has_minor_title = title_court_jester }
			save_event_target_as = jester
		}

	#Fake leg
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTmaimed404_A

		add_character_modifier = { name = prosthetic_leg duration = -1 }
	}

		name = EVTOPTmaimed404_B
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTmaimed404_B_TOOLTIP }

		scaled_wealth = { value = -0.20 min = -20 }

		add_character_modifier = { name = peg_leg duration = -1 }

		hidden_tooltip = { random = { chance = 50 give_nickname = nick_peg_leg } }
	}

	#No prosthetic leg

		name = EVTOPTmaimed404_C
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTmaimed404_C_TOOLTIP }
	}
}

# maimed.500 Phantom pain
# The lost limb, eye or nose aches: ask the court physician.
# Scope: ROOT is the maimed character. Fired by maimed.100, maimed.200,
# maimed.300, maimed.400.

	id = maimed.500
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion

	desc = { trigger = { trait = one_eyed } text = EVTDESCmaimed500_EYE }
	desc = { trigger = { trait = one_handed } text = EVTDESCmaimed500_HAND }
	desc = { trigger = { trait = one_legged } text = EVTDESCmaimed500_LEG }

	is_triggered_only = yes
	hide_from = yes

	immediate = {
		any_courtier_or_vassal = {
			limit = { has_minor_title = title_court_physician }
			save_event_target_as = rip_physician
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTmaimed500_A

		add_character_modifier = { name = phantom_pain duration = -1 }

		hidden_tooltip = { character_event = { id = maimed.501 days = 30 random = 60 } }

# maimed.501 Two treatments

		has_character_modifier = phantom_pain

		name = EVTOPTmaimed501_A

			50 = {
				character_event = { id = maimed.502 tooltip = EVTOPTmaimed501_A_SUCCESS days = 30 }
			}
			50 = {
				character_event = { id = maimed.503 tooltip = EVTOPTmaimed501_A_FAILURE days = 15 }
			} #Gives food poisoning
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTmaimed501_B

		random_list = {
			80 = {
				character_event = { id = maimed.502 tooltip = EVTOPTmaimed501_A_SUCCESS days = 30 }
			}
			20 = {
				character_event = { id = maimed.504 tooltip = EVTOPTmaimed501_B_FAILURE days = 15 }
			} #Gives infirm
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTmaimed501_C
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTmaimed501_C_TOOLTIP }
	}

###############################################################################
# Nursing the injured (maimed.505-511)
###############################################################################

# maimed.511 Someone to care for you
# Hidden. A badly hurt character's spouse, lover or child may offer to nurse
# them (505).
# Scope: ROOT is the injured character. Fired by the on_yearly_pulse random
# list.
character_event = {
	id = maimed.511

	is_triggered_only = yes

	hide_window = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			any_child = { is_alive = yes }
			any_lover = { is_alive = yes }
			any_spouse = { is_alive = yes }
		}
		OR = {
			trait = severely_injured
			AND = {
				has_character_modifier = bedridden_illness
				OR = { trait = gout trait = cancer trait = has_typhoid_fever trait = flu }
			}
		}
	}

	option = {
		random_spouse = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_minor_title = title_court_physician }
				NOT = { is_lover = ROOT }
				block_general_event_trigger = no
			}
			save_event_target_as = caretaker
		}

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { event_target:caretaker = { always = yes } } }
			random_child = {
				limit = {
					liege = { character = ROOT }
					age = 16
					NOT = { has_minor_title = title_court_physician }
				}
				save_event_target_as = caretaker
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { event_target:caretaker = { always = yes } } }
			random_lover = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_minor_title = title_court_physician }
					block_general_event_trigger = no
				}
				save_event_target_as = caretaker
			}
		}

		event_target:caretaker = { character_event = { id = maimed.505 } }
	}
}

# maimed.505 Care for them?
# A close relation decides whether to nurse the injured.
# Scope: ROOT is the carer, FROM the injured. Fired by maimed.511.
character_event = {
	id = maimed.505
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	picture = GFX_evt_doctor_tending_to_sick

	desc = { trigger = { FROM = { trait = severely_injured } } text = EVTDESCmaimed505_A }
	desc = { trigger = { FROM = { NOT = { trait = severely_injured } } } text = EVTDESCmaimed505_B }

	is_triggered_only = yes

	#Yes
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTmaimed505_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTmaimed505_A_TOOLTIP }

		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = maimed.506 } } }

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 60

			modifier = { trait = kind factor = 2 }
			modifier = { opinion = { who = FROM value = 40 } factor = 2 }
		}
	}

	#Yes, but badly...
	option = {
		trigger = { NOT = { opinion = { who = FROM value = 20 } } }

		name = EVTOPTmaimed505_B
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTmaimed505_B_TOOLTIP }

		set_character_flag = bad_caretaker
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = maimed.506 } } }

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 15

			modifier = { trait = ambitious factor = 2 }
			modifier = { trait = cruel factor = 2 }
			modifier = { trait = deceitful factor = 2 }
			modifier = { trait = honest factor = 0.5 }
			modifier = { NOT = { opinion = { who = FROM value = -20 } } factor = 2 }
		}
	}

	#No
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTmaimed505_C

		ai_chance = { factor = 40 }
	}
}

# maimed.506 An offer of care
# The injured hears the offer.
# Scope: ROOT is the injured, FROM the carer. Fired by maimed.505.
character_event = {
	id = maimed.506
	desc = EVTDESCmaimed506
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	picture = GFX_evt_doctor_tending_to_sick

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			trait = severely_injured
			AND = {
				has_character_modifier = bedridden_illness
				OR = { trait = gout trait = cancer trait = has_typhoid_fever trait = flu }
			}
		}
	}

	#Yes
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTmaimed506_A
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTOPTmaimed506_A_TOOLTIP }

		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_list = {
				60 = {
					modifier = { factor = 1.3 physician_good_trigger = yes }
					modifier = { factor = 1.3 is_smart_trigger = yes }
					modifier = { factor = 1.3 learning = 12 }

					character_event = { id = maimed.507 days = 14 }
				}
				40 = {
					modifier = { factor = 5 FROM = { has_character_flag = bad_caretaker } }
					modifier = { factor = 1.3 physician_bad_trigger = yes }
					modifier = { factor = 1.3 is_dumb_trigger = yes }
					modifier = { factor = 1.3 NOT = { learning = 8 } }

					character_event = { id = maimed.509 days = 14 }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	#No
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTmaimed506_B

		prestige = 20

		reverse_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_ungrateful years = 3 }
	}
}

# maimed.507 Good care
# The carer is attentive.
# Scope: ROOT is the injured, FROMFROM the carer. Fired by maimed.506.
character_event = {
	id = maimed.507
	desc = EVTDESCmaimed507
	border = GFX_event_normal_frame_religion
	picture = GFX_evt_doctor_tending_to_sick

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTmaimed507_A

		add_character_modifier = { name = good_care duration = 365 }

		event_target:caretaker = { character_event = { id = maimed.508 } }

		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { event_target:caretaker = { is_child_of = ROOT } }
				NOT = { is_lover = event_target:caretaker }
				NOT = { event_target:caretaker = { has_character_flag = bad_caretaker } }
			}

			event_target:caretaker = { set_character_flag = make_lover }
			tooltip = { add_lover = event_target:caretaker }
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				event_target:caretaker = { is_child_of = ROOT }
				NOT = { is_friend = event_target:caretaker }
				NOT = { event_target:caretaker = { has_character_flag = bad_caretaker } }
			}

			event_target:caretaker = { set_character_flag = make_friend }
			tooltip = { add_friend = event_target:caretaker }
		}

		tooltip = {
			opinion = { who = event_target:caretaker modifier = opinion_grateful years = 10 }
		}
	}
}

			text = EVTOPTmaimed508_A
		}
		name = { trigger = { has_character_flag = bad_caretaker } text = EVTOPTmaimed508_B }

		clr_character_flag = bad_caretaker

		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = make_lover }

			clr_character_flag = make_lover
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character_flag = make_friend }
			add_lover = FROM
			clr_character_flag = make_lover
		}

		reverse_opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_grateful years = 10 }
	}
}

###############################################################################
# Physique events
# Namespace: physique (was HFP.20600 - HFP.20703 in HFP_health_events.txt)
# Faction: Generic
# Serves: every living race; ogres, halflings, elves and the servants of
#   Nurgle eat by their own rules
#
# Weight. Every five years physique.10 adds up what a character eats, drinks
# and does into physique_variable (fat at 15, malnourished at -15; see the
# weight effects in 00_scripted_effects.txt). A character on the way to
# either may stop and think about why (physique.20, 50), loved ones step in,
# friends give good or bad advice, and the worst cases turn into obesity,
# wasting, or a scandal at court. The dead and the daemonic never change,
# nor do greenskins, Skaven, Lizardmen, beastmen, Fimir and beasts
# (physique_weight_changes_trigger).
#
# For an ogre a great gut is rank and worship, and Nurgle blesses a swollen
# belly (physique_gut_is_glory_trigger): they are never warned off food,
# growing fat wins them prestige or piety, and wasting shames an ogre.
# Halflings and Nurglites fatten a little faster, ogres faster still, elves
# a little slower. The temperance faiths (the Old World temples, Ormazd and
# the Dragon faith, physique_temperance_faith_trigger) scold gluttons and
# fast.
#
# Notifications                    physique.1 - physique.4
# The five-year tally              physique.10
# Growing fat                      physique.20 - physique.40
# Growing thin                     physique.50 - physique.71
# Health warnings                  physique.80 - physique.81
# A friend's advice                physique.90 - physique.101
# Obese and wasting                physique.110 - physique.111
# Sturdy and feeble youths         physique.120 - physique.126
# The Leechlord                    physique.130 - physique.138
# The Whisperer                    physique.140 - physique.150
# The Black Boar                   physique.160 - physique.167
# The ogre's great gut             physique.170
#
# Callers: physique.10 and the five-year random list (20, 50, 80, 81, 90)
#   and the decade list (110, 111) in 00_on_actions; 1-4, 20, 22-26, 38-40
#   and 50 from the weight effects in 00_scripted_effects.txt; 120 from the
#   adolescence events (ze_adolescence_events); 170 from on_startup.
# Saved: variable physique_variable; flags flag_primed_physique,
#   flag_becoming_obese, flag_becoming_underweight and the fat/malnourished
#   reason flags (cleared by clear_fat_flags_effect and
#   clear_malnourished_flags_effect); modifiers obese_modifier,
#   severely_underweight_modifier, leechlord_modifier, whisperer_modifier,
#   black_boar_modifier.
###############################################################################

namespace = physique

###############################################################################
# Notifications
###############################################################################

# physique.1 Fat
# The character has become fat. An ogre wins prestige for it and a
# servant of Nurgle piety.
# Scope: ROOT is the character. Fired from weight_trait_check_effect.
character_event = {
	id = physique.1
	desc = { text = EVTDESCphysique1 trigger = { physique_gut_is_glory_trigger = no } }
	desc = {
		text = EVTDESCphysique1_ogre
		trigger = { physique_gut_is_glory_trigger = yes NOT = { religion = nurgle } }
	}
	desc = { text = EVTDESCphysique1_nurgle trigger = { religion = nurgle } }
	picture = GFX_evt_fancy_feast

	notification = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAphysique1
		trigger = { physique_gut_is_glory_trigger = no }
	}
	option = { #The Maw is pleased
		name = EVTOPTBphysique1
		trigger = { physique_gut_is_glory_trigger = yes NOT = { religion = nurgle } }
		prestige = 50
	}
	option = { #Grandfather's blessing
		name = EVTOPTCphysique1
		trigger = { religion = nurgle }
		piety = 25
	}
}

# The five-year tally

# physique.10 The five-year tally
# Hidden. Adds what the character's traits, focus, modifiers and station do
# to physique_variable, may prime a flavour event and then checks the
# weight traits. Only for races whose shape follows their food
# (physique_weight_changes_trigger).
# Scope: ROOT is the character. Fired from on_five_year_pulse.
character_event = {
	id = physique.10
	hide_window = yes

	min_age = 11
	max_age = 55

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		physique_weight_changes_trigger = yes
	}

	immediate = {
		#Traits
		if = {
			limit = {
				trait = indulgent_wastrel
				OR = { prisoner = no has_character_modifier = house_arrest }
			}
			change_variable = { which = physique_variable value = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				trait = tough_soldier
				check_variable = { which = physique_variable value = 1 }
			}
			change_variable = { which = physique_variable value = -1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = haughty }
			change_variable = { which = physique_variable value = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = timid }
			change_variable = { which = physique_variable value = -1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = rowdy check_variable = { which = physique_variable value = 1 } }
			change_variable = { which = physique_variable value = -1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				trait = indolent
				OR = { prisoner = no has_character_modifier = house_arrest }
			}
			change_variable = { which = physique_variable value = 2 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = playful check_variable = { which = physique_variable value = 1 } }
			change_variable = { which = physique_variable value = -1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = fussy }
			change_variable = { which = physique_variable value = -2 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				trait = stressed
				trait = gluttonous
				OR = { prisoner = no has_character_modifier = house_arrest }
			}
			change_variable = { which = physique_variable value = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = stressed trait = temperate }
			change_variable = { which = physique_variable value = -1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = stressed NOR = { trait = temperate trait = gluttonous } }
			random_list = {
				50 = { change_variable = { which = physique_variable value = -1 } }
				50 = { change_variable = { which = physique_variable value = 1 } }
				10 = { }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				trait = depressed
				trait = gluttonous
				OR = { prisoner = no has_character_modifier = house_arrest }
			}
			change_variable = { which = physique_variable value = 2 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = depressed trait = temperate }
			change_variable = { which = physique_variable value = -2 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = depressed NOR = { trait = temperate trait = gluttonous } }
			random_list = {
				50 = { change_variable = { which = physique_variable value = -2 } }
				50 = { change_variable = { which = physique_variable value = 2 } }
				10 = { }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = wroth NOR = { trait = temperate trait = gluttonous } }
			random_list = {
				50 = { change_variable = { which = physique_variable value = -1 } }
				50 = { change_variable = { which = physique_variable value = 1 } }
				10 = { }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = maimed }
			change_variable = { which = physique_variable value = -1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = infirm }
			change_variable = { which = physique_variable value = -1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = incapable }
			change_variable = { which = physique_variable value = -4 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = drunkard }
			change_variable = { which = physique_variable value = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = physician check_variable = { which = physique_variable value = 1 } }
			change_variable = { which = physique_variable value = -1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				trait = physician
				NOT = { check_variable = { which = physique_variable value = -1 } }
			}
			change_variable = { which = physique_variable value = 1 }
		}
		if = { limit = { trait = ill } change_variable = { which = physique_variable value = -1 } }
		if = {
			limit = { trait = pneumonic }
			change_variable = { which = physique_variable value = -2 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { trait = syphilitic }
			change_variable = { which = physique_variable value = -2 }
		}

				NOT = { has_character_modifier = hard_diet }
				OR = {
					has_character_modifier = coping_glutton
					has_character_modifier = stress_relief
					has_character_modifier = anti_depression
					has_character_modifier = munificent_modifier
					has_character_modifier = jolly_matron_modifier
					has_character_modifier = jolly_patriarch_modifier
				}
			}
			random_list = {
				30 = {
					modifier = { factor = 0.8 trait = slothful }
					modifier = { factor = 0.8 trait = gluttonous }
					# Nothing
				}
				50 = { change_variable = { which = physique_variable value = 1 } }
				20 = {
					modifier = { factor = 0.8 trait = diligent }
					modifier = { factor = 0.8 trait = temperate }
					change_variable = { which = physique_variable value = 2 }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				trait = is_malnourished
				NOT = { has_character_modifier = hard_diet }
				OR = {
					has_character_modifier = coping_glutton
					has_character_modifier = stress_relief
					has_character_modifier = anti_depression
					has_character_modifier = extremely_temperate
					has_character_modifier = restless_study
					has_character_modifier = restless_prayer
					has_character_modifier = malnourished_paranoia
					has_character_modifier = tireless_worker
				}
			}
			random_list = {
				30 = {
					modifier = { factor = 0.8 trait = slothful }
					modifier = { factor = 0.8 trait = gluttonous }
					# Nothing
				}
				50 = { change_variable = { which = physique_variable value = -1 } }
				20 = {
					modifier = { factor = 0.8 trait = diligent }
					modifier = { factor = 0.8 trait = temperate }
					change_variable = { which = physique_variable value = -2 }
				}
			}
		}

		weight_flavor_conditions_effect = yes #Fire flavor event if trigger has been met.
		#####################################################
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_character_flag = flag_primed_physique } }
			weight_trait_check_effect = yes #Apply or remove either of the traits, depending on resulting score, if no random flavor is about to fire.
		}
	}
}
