# physique.134 Cold blood
# Time has passed. The leeching has made the liege cold and detached. Hang
# the physician, keep him, or stop the treatment.
# Scope: ROOT is the liege, FROMFROM the physician. Fired from physique.131.
character_event = {
	id = physique.134
	desc = EVTDESCphysique134_a
	picture = GFX_evt_died_old_age
	show_from_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { #Do not care, but kill physician.
		name = EVTOPTAphysique134
		trigger = { trait = paranoid }
		tooltip_info = paranoid
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = cruel } } add_trait = cruel }
		add_character_modifier = { modifier = leechlord_modifier duration = -1 }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_nickname = yes } }
			ROOT = { give_nickname = nick_the_leechlord }
		}
		tooltip = {
			FROMFROM = {
				if = { limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes } antideath_effect = yes }
				else = { death = { death_reason = death_execution_hanging killer = ROOT } }
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = { FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = physique.135 } } } #Actually kill Physician.
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
	option = { #Do not care.
		name = EVTOPTBphysique134
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = cruel } } add_trait = cruel }
		add_character_modifier = { modifier = leechlord_modifier duration = -1 }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_nickname = yes } }
			ROOT = { give_nickname = nick_the_leechlord }
		}
		opinion = { who = FROMFROM modifier = opinion_trusted_servant months = 1200 }
		hidden_tooltip = { FROMFROM = { character_event = { id = physique.137 days = 17 } } } #Notify Physician.
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
	}
	option = { #Stop treatment.
		name = EVTOPTCphysique134
		gain_weight_medium_effect = yes
		remove_opinion = { who = FROMFROM modifier = opinion_trusted_servant }
		custom_tooltip = { text = tooltip_health_deteriorates }
		hidden_tooltip = { health = -0.5 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}
}

# physique.144 A complaint
# The wife complains. Have the vassal kill her, set her aside, or free him
# of his vow.
# Scope: ROOT is the liege, FROM the wife, FROMFROMFROM the vassal. Fired from
#   physique.143.
character_event = {
	id = physique.144
	desc = EVTDESCphysique144_a
	picture = GFX_evt_scandal

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { #Make vassal kill wife.
		name = EVTOPTAphysique144
		trigger = { trait = cruel }
		tooltip_info = cruel
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = deceitful } } add_trait = deceitful }
		add_character_modifier = { modifier = whisperer_modifier duration = -1 }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_nickname = yes } }
			ROOT = { give_nickname = nick_the_whisperer }
		}
		opinion = { who = FROMFROMFROM modifier = opinion_trusted_servant months = 1200 }
		tooltip = {
			FROM = {
				if = { limit = { is_invincible_trigger = yes } antideath_effect = yes }
				else = { death = { death_reason = death_murder_unknown } }
			}
		}
		hidden_tooltip = { FROMFROMFROM = { character_event = { id = physique.145 } } } #Vassal obeys.
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
	option = { #Make vassal repudiate wife.
		name = EVTOPTBphysique144
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = deceitful } } add_trait = deceitful }
		add_character_modifier = { modifier = whisperer_modifier duration = -1 }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_nickname = yes } }
			ROOT = { give_nickname = nick_the_whisperer }
		}
		opinion = { who = FROMFROMFROM modifier = opinion_trusted_servant months = 1200 }
		hidden_tooltip = { FROMFROMFROM = { character_event = { id = physique.147 } } } #Vassal obeys.
		ai_chance = { factor = 50 }
	}
	option = { #Free vassal of vow.
		name = EVTOPTCphysique144
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = kind } } add_trait = kind }
		gain_weight_medium_effect = yes
		remove_opinion = { who = FROMFROMFROM modifier = opinion_trusted_servant }
		custom_tooltip = { text = tooltip_health_deteriorates }
		hidden_tooltip = { health = -0.5 }
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}
}

# physique.161 The song
# The liege hears the song. Laugh, flog the bard, or roast him.

	picture = GFX_evt_jugglers
	hide_from = yes

		FROM = { is_alive = yes trait = is_fat }
	}

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { #Cook the bard.
		name = EVTOPTAphysique161
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTTOOLTIPphysique161_a }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = cruel } } add_trait = cruel }
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = physique.166 } } } #Notify vassal.
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
	option = { #Punish bard lightly.
		name = EVTOPTBphysique161
		custom_tooltip = { text = EVTTOOLTIPphysique161_b }
		if = { limit = { trait = just } change_stewardship = 1 }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = just } } add_trait = just }
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = physique.164 } } } #Notify vassal.
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
	option = { #Tolerate bard.
		name = EVTOPTCphysique161
		if = { limit = { trait = kind } change_diplomacy = 1 }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { trait = kind } } add_trait = kind }
		FROM = {
			show_scope_change = no
			opinion = { who = ROOT modifier = opinion_swayed_2 years = 15 }
		}
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = physique.162 } } } #Notify vassal.
		ai_chance = { factor = 0 }
	}
}

	picture = GFX_evt_jugglers

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { #
		name = EVTOPTAphysique162
		opinion = { who = FROM modifier = opinion_swayed_2 years = 15 }
		hidden_tooltip = { FROM = { character_event = { id = physique.163 } } } #Notify ruler.
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
}

# physique.167 The Black Boar
# The roasted bard is carried into the hall. Eat him and turn cannibal, or
# not. Piety is lost unless the faith is the Great Maw's or the Dark Gods'.
# Scope: ROOT is the liege. Fired from physique.166.
character_event = {
	id = physique.167
	desc = EVTDESCphysique167_a
	picture = GFX_evt_jugglers
	hide_from = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	option = { #Be(come) Cannibal.
		name = EVTOPTAphysique167

		add_trait = cannibal_trait
		add_character_modifier = { modifier = black_boar_modifier duration = -1 }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_nickname = yes } }
			ROOT = { give_nickname = nick_the_black_boar }
		}

		gain_weight_medium_effect = yes
		if = { #The Great Maw and the Dark Gods care nothing for a bard
			limit = { physique_man_eating_faith_trigger = no }
			tiered_piety_negative_effect = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
	option = { #Good.
		name = EVTOPTBphysique167
		trigger = { NOT = { trait = cannibal_trait } }
		add_character_modifier = { modifier = black_boar_modifier duration = -1 }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_nickname = yes } }
			ROOT = { give_nickname = nick_the_black_boar }
		}
		if = { #The Great Maw and the Dark Gods care nothing for a bard
			limit = { physique_man_eating_faith_trigger = no }
			tiered_piety_negative_effect = yes
		}
		ai_chance = { factor = 100 }
	}
}

# physique.170 The ogre's great gut
# Hidden. Keeps the great gut modifier (+20 combat rating) in step with the
# is_fat trait for ogres whose weight changed outside the five-year tally
# (history, other events) at game start, and takes it away if the trait is
# gone.
# The five-year tally keeps it in step afterwards (weight_trait_check_effect).
# Scope: ROOT is the character. Fired from on_startup.
character_event = {
	id = physique.170
	hide_window = yes

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		OR = { trait = creature_ogre has_character_modifier = physique_ogre_great_gut }
	}

	immediate = {
		physique_ogre_gut_sync_effect = yes
	}
}
